  8054: // Patches one local AArch64 branch after its target has been emitted. The
  8055: // canonical emitter uses this for the fixed-shape loops inside one IR row;
  8056: // ordinary IR block branches continue through CCanonicalBranchPatch below.
  8057: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(CodegenBuffer* buffer, u32 branch_offset, u32 target_offset, bool conditional)
  8058: {
  8059:     bool result = false;
  8060:     if (buffer && buffer->error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE && branch_offset <= UINT32_MAX - 4 && branch_offset + 4 <= buffer->count && target_offset <= UINT32_MAX)
  8061:     {
  8062:         s64 delta = (s64)target_offset - (s64)branch_offset;
  8063:         s64 minimum = conditional ? -(s64)(1 << 20) : -(s64)(1 << 27);
  8064:         s64 maximum = conditional ? (s64)(1 << 20) : (s64)(1 << 27);
  8065:         if ((delta & 3) || delta < minimum || delta >= maximum)
  8066:         {
  8067:             buffer->error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
  8068:         }
  8069:         else
  8070:         {
  8071:             u32 instruction = 0;
  8072:             memcpy(&instruction, buffer->bytes + branch_offset, sizeof(instruction));
  8073:             u32 immediate_mask = conditional ? UINT32_C(0x00ffffe0) : UINT32_C(0x03ffffff);
  8074:             if (instruction & immediate_mask)
  8075:             {
  8076:                 // A local patch must start from the zero-immediate template;
  8077:                 // silently OR-ing onto an old displacement would corrupt the
  8078:                 // loop target while still producing a decodable branch.
  8079:                 buffer->error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
  8080:             }
  8081:             else
  8082:             {
  8083:                 u32 immediate = (u32)(delta >> 2);
  8084:                 instruction |= conditional ? (immediate & 0x7ffff) << 5 : immediate & 0x03ffffff;
  8085:                 memcpy(buffer->bytes + branch_offset, &instruction, sizeof(instruction));
  8086:                 result = true;
  8087:             }
  8088:         }
  8089:     }
  8090:     else if (buffer && buffer->error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE)
  8091:     {
  8092:         buffer->error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
  8093:     }
  8094:     return result;
  8095: }

  8097: // Emits the canonical 128-bit divide/remainder loop. Inputs arrive in
  8098: // x9:x10 (dividend) and x11:x12 (divisor), low eightbyte first. The loop
  8099: // shifts one dividend bit into x13:x14, subtracts the divisor when the
  8100: // partial remainder is large enough, and accumulates the quotient in x0:x1.
  8101: // Signed operations normalize both operands before the loop and restore the
  8102: // quotient/remainder sign afterwards; unsigned operations use the same loop
  8103: // without that normalization. On success the selected result is x9:x10.
  8104: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool codegen_canonical_a64_i128_divide(CodegenBuffer* buffer, bool signed_division, bool remainder_result)
  8105: {
  8106:     bool result = false;
  8107:     if (buffer)
  8108:     {
  8109:         if (signed_division)
  8110:         {
  8111:             // x15/x16 retain the operand sign masks; the pair XOR/subtract
  8112:             // turns each negative operand into its unsigned magnitude.
  8113:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x937ffd4f); // asr x15, x10, #63
  8114:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca0f0129); // eor x9, x9, x15
  8115:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca0f014a); // eor x10, x10, x15
  8116:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xeb0f0129); // subs x9, x9, x15
  8117:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xda0f014a); // sbc x10, x10, x15
  8118:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x937ffd90); // asr x16, x12, #63
  8119:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca10016b); // eor x11, x11, x16
  8120:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca10018c); // eor x12, x12, x16
  8121:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xeb10016b); // subs x11, x11, x16
  8122:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xda10018c); // sbc x12, x12, x16
  8123:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca1001f1); // eor x17, x15, x16
  8124:         }
  8125: 
  8126:         // x0:x1 quotient, x13:x14 partial remainder, x2 iteration count.
  8127:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa1f03e0); // mov x0, xzr
  8128:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa1f03e1); // mov x1, xzr
  8129:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa1f03ed); // mov x13, xzr
  8130:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa1f03ee); // mov x14, xzr
  8131:         a64_emit_constant_compact(buffer, 2, 128);
  8132:         u32 loop_offset = (u32)buffer->count;
  8133:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xab090129); // adds x9, x9, x9
  8134:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xba0a014a); // adcs x10, x10, x10
  8135:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xba0d01ad); // adcs x13, x13, x13
  8136:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xba0e01ce); // adcs x14, x14, x14
  8137:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xab000000); // adds x0, x0, x0
  8138:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xba010021); // adcs x1, x1, x1
  8139:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xeb0c01df); // cmp x14, x12
  8140:         u32 high_less_branch = (u32)buffer->count;
  8141:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000003); // b.lo skip_subtract
  8142:         u32 high_greater_branch = (u32)buffer->count;
  8143:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000008); // b.hi subtract
  8144:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xeb0b01bf); // cmp x13, x11
  8145:         u32 low_less_branch = (u32)buffer->count;
  8146:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000003); // b.lo skip_subtract
  8147:         u32 subtract_offset = (u32)buffer->count;
  8148:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xeb0b01ad); // subs x13, x13, x11
  8149:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xda0c01ce); // sbc x14, x14, x12
  8150:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xb2400000); // orr x0, x0, #1
  8151:         u32 skip_subtract = (u32)buffer->count;
  8152:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf1000442); // subs x2, x2, #1
  8153:         u32 loop_branch = (u32)buffer->count;
  8154:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000001); // b.ne loop
  8155:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, high_less_branch, skip_subtract, true);
  8156:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, high_greater_branch, subtract_offset, true);
  8157:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, low_less_branch, skip_subtract, true);
  8158:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, loop_branch, loop_offset, true);
  8159: 
  8160:         if (signed_division)
  8161:         {
  8162:             if (remainder_result)
  8163:             {
  8164:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca0f01ad); // eor x13, x13, x15
  8165:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca0f01ce); // eor x14, x14, x15
  8166:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xeb0f01ad); // subs x13, x13, x15
  8167:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xda0f01ce); // sbc x14, x14, x15
  8168:             }
  8169:             else
  8170:             {
  8171:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca110000); // eor x0, x0, x17
  8172:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca110021); // eor x1, x1, x17
  8173:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xeb110000); // subs x0, x0, x17
  8174:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xda110021); // sbc x1, x1, x17
  8175:             }
  8176:         }
  8177:         if (remainder_result)
  8178:         {
  8179:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa0d03e9); // mov x9, x13
  8180:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa0e03ea); // mov x10, x14
  8181:         }
  8182:         else
  8183:         {
  8184:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa0003e9); // mov x9, x0
  8185:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa0103ea); // mov x10, x1
  8186:         }
  8187:         result = buffer->error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE;
  8188:     }
  8189:     return result;
  8190: }
  8191: 
  8192: // Converts an i128 pair in a frame slot to an AArch64 scalar FP value. The
  8193: // halves are converted as an unsigned magnitude, with an integer sticky-bit
  8194: // combine before the final double conversion. Converting the halves
  8195: // independently and adding them can double-round a halfway value (the low
  8196: // half's sticky bit would have been lost by ucvtf), so the common path first
  8197: // rounds a target-precision significand (53 bits for binary64, 24 for binary32)
  8198: // and scales it by the discarded-bit count.
  8199: // Signed inputs are made positive first and negated in FP after the magnitude
  8200: // has been formed. The final narrowing is done only once when the requested
  8201: // destination is float.
  8202: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool codegen_canonical_a64_i128_to_float(CodegenBuffer* buffer, u32 source_offset, u32 result_offset, u32 target_width,
  8203:                                                             bool signed_value)
  8204: {
  8205:     bool result = false;
  8206:     if (buffer && (target_width == 32 || target_width == 64) &&
  8207:         codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(buffer, 9, source_offset, 8, false, false) &&
  8208:         codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(buffer, 10, source_offset + 8, 8, false, false))
  8209:     {
  8210:         if (signed_value)
  8211:         {
  8212:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x937ffd4f); // asr x15, x10, #63
  8213:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca0f012d); // eor x13, x9, x15
  8214:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca0f014e); // eor x14, x10, x15
  8215:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xeb0f01ad); // subs x13, x13, x15
  8216:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xda0f01ce); // sbc x14, x14, x15
  8217:         }
  8218:         else
  8219:         {
  8220:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa0903ed); // mov x13, x9
  8221:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa0a03ee); // mov x14, x10
  8222:         }
  8223:         // A zero high half is already a 64-bit conversion. Keeping it on a
  8224:         // separate path also avoids asking the pair-significand code below to
  8225:         // represent a shift outside the target format's range.
  8226:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf10001df); // cmp x14, #0
  8227:         u32 direct_branch = (u32)buffer->count;
  8228:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000001); // b.ne general
  8229: 
  8230:         // For a nonzero high half, E = floor(log2(value)) = 64 + h and the
  8231:         // target conversion discards shift = E - (p - 1) bits. x0 is the
  8232:         // high-half leading-zero count, x2 the discard count. The three paths
  8233:         // below form the top p bits (x0) and the round/sticky pair (x1/x4);
  8234:         // all then join at one nearest-even increment.
  8235:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa0d03e0); // mov x0, x13
  8236:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa1f03e2); // mov x2, xzr
  8237:         u32 direct_to_convert = (u32)buffer->count;
  8238:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x14000000); // b convert
  8239: 
  8240:         u32 general_offset = (u32)buffer->count;
  8241:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xdac011c0); // clz x0, x14
  8242:         // p includes the hidden leading bit: 53 for binary64 and 24 for
  8243:         // binary32. Thus shift = (64 + h) - (p - 1) = bias - clz(high).
  8244:         a64_emit_constant_compact(buffer, 2, target_width == 64 ? 75 : 104);
  8245:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xcb000042); // sub x2, x2, x0
  8246:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf101005f); // cmp x2, #64
  8247:         u32 low_branch = (u32)buffer->count;
  8248:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000003); // b.lo low_path
  8249:         u32 shift64_branch = (u32)buffer->count;
  8250:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000000); // b.eq shift64_path
  8251: 
  8252:         // shift > 64: the significand is high >> (shift - 64), the round bit
  8253:         // is immediately below it, and sticky includes the low half plus all
  8254:         // lower high-half bits. x3 is shift - 64 and x4 is the round-bit
  8255:         // position in the high half.
  8256:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xd1010043); // sub x3, x2, #64
  8257:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac325c0); // lsr x0, x14, x3
  8258:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xd1000464); // sub x4, x3, #1
  8259:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac425c1); // lsr x1, x14, x4
  8260:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x92400021); // and x1, x1, #1
  8261:         a64_emit_constant_compact(buffer, 5, 64);
  8262:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xcb0400a5); // sub x5, x5, x4
  8263:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac521c5); // lsl x5, x14, x5
  8264:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf100009f); // cmp x4, #0
  8265:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9a8503e5); // csel x5, xzr, x5, eq
  8266:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf10000bf); // cmp x5, #0
  8267:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9a9f07e4); // cset x4, ne
  8268:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf10001bf); // cmp x13, #0
  8269:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9a9f07e5); // cset x5, ne
  8270:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa050084); // orr x4, x4, x5
  8271:         u32 high_to_round = (u32)buffer->count;
  8272:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x14000000); // b round
  8273: 
  8274:         u32 shift64_offset = (u32)buffer->count;
  8275:         // shift == 64: high is the candidate significand, bit 63 of low is
  8276:         // the round bit, and the remaining low bits are sticky.
  8277:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa0e03e0); // mov x0, x14
  8278:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xd37ffda1); // lsr x1, x13, #63
  8279:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x92400021); // and x1, x1, #1
  8280:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xd37ff9a4); // lsl x4, x13, #1
  8281:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf100009f); // cmp x4, #0
  8282:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9a9f07e4); // cset x4, ne
  8283:         u32 shift64_to_round = (u32)buffer->count;
  8284:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x14000000); // b round
  8285: 
  8286:         u32 low_offset = (u32)buffer->count;
  8287:         // shift < 64: combine the top of high with low >> shift. Sticky is
  8288:         // all low bits below the round bit.
  8289:         a64_emit_constant_compact(buffer, 3, 64);
  8290:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xcb020063); // sub x3, x3, x2
  8291:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac321c0); // lsl x0, x14, x3
  8292:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac225a1); // lsr x1, x13, x2
  8293:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa010000); // orr x0, x0, x1
  8294:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xd1000443); // sub x3, x2, #1
  8295:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac325a1); // lsr x1, x13, x3
  8296:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x92400021); // and x1, x1, #1
  8297:         a64_emit_constant_compact(buffer, 4, 65);
  8298:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xcb020084); // sub x4, x4, x2
  8299:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac421a4); // lsl x4, x13, x4
  8300:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf100009f); // cmp x4, #0
  8301:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9a9f07e4); // cset x4, ne
  8302:         u32 low_to_round = (u32)buffer->count;
  8303:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x14000000); // b round
  8304: 
  8305:         u32 round_offset = (u32)buffer->count;
  8306:         // Round to nearest, ties to even. x1 is the round bit, x4 the sticky
  8307:         // flag, and x0's low bit supplies the tie parity.
  8308:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x92400005); // and x5, x0, #1
  8309:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa050084); // orr x4, x4, x5
  8310:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x8a010084); // and x4, x4, x1
  8311:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x91000405); // add x5, x0, #1
  8312:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf100009f); // cmp x4, #0
  8313:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9a8010a0); // csel x0, x5, x0, ne
  8314: 
  8315:         u32 convert_offset = (u32)buffer->count;
  8316:         if (target_width == 32)
  8317:         {
  8318:             // x0 has at most 24 significant bits here, so this conversion is
  8319:             // exact. Scale in binary32 directly; narrowing a rounded binary64
  8320:             // value would introduce a second rounding step.
  8321:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9e230000); // ucvtf s0, x0
  8322:             a64_emit_constant(buffer, 16, UINT64_C(0x3f800000)); // float 1.0
  8323:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x8b025e10); // add x16, x16, x2, lsl #23
  8324:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e270201); // fmov s1, w16
  8325:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e210800); // fmul s0, s0, s1
  8326:         }
  8327:         else
  8328:         {
  8329:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9e630000); // ucvtf d0, x0
  8330:             a64_emit_constant(buffer, 16, UINT64_C(0x3ff0000000000000)); // 1.0
  8331:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x8b02d210); // add x16, x16, x2, lsl #52
  8332:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9e670201); // fmov d1, x16
  8333:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e610800); // fmul d0, d0, d1
  8334:         }
  8335: 
  8336:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, direct_branch, general_offset, true);
  8337:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, direct_to_convert, convert_offset, false);
  8338:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, low_branch, low_offset, true);
  8339:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, shift64_branch, shift64_offset, true);
  8340:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, high_to_round, round_offset, false);
  8341:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, shift64_to_round, round_offset, false);
  8342:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, low_to_round, round_offset, false);
  8343:         if (target_width == 32)
  8344:         {
  8345:             if (signed_value)
  8346:             {
  8347:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf10001ff); // cmp x15, #0
  8348:                 u32 skip_negate = (u32)buffer->count;
  8349:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000000); // b.eq skip_negate
  8350:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e214000); // fneg s0, s0
  8351:                 u32 after_negate = (u32)buffer->count;
  8352:                 codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, skip_negate, after_negate, true);
  8353:             }
  8354:             result = codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(buffer, 0, result_offset, 4, true);
  8355:         }
  8356:         else
  8357:         {
  8358:             if (signed_value)
  8359:             {
  8360:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf10001ff); // cmp x15, #0
  8361:                 u32 skip_negate = (u32)buffer->count;
  8362:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000000); // b.eq skip_negate
  8363:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e614000); // fneg d0, d0

  8222:         }

  8224:         // separate path also avoids asking the pair-significand code below to
  8225:         // represent a shift outside the target format's range.
  8226:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf10001df); // cmp x14, #0
  8227:         u32 direct_branch = (u32)buffer->count;
  8228:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000001); // b.ne general
  8229: 
  8230:         // For a nonzero high half, E = floor(log2(value)) = 64 + h and the
  8231:         // target conversion discards shift = E - (p - 1) bits. x0 is the
  8232:         // high-half leading-zero count, x2 the discard count. The three paths
  8233:         // below form the top p bits (x0) and the round/sticky pair (x1/x4);
  8234:         // all then join at one nearest-even increment.
  8235:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa0d03e0); // mov x0, x13
  8236:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa1f03e2); // mov x2, xzr

  8238:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x14000000); // b convert
  8239: 
  8240:         u32 general_offset = (u32)buffer->count;
  8241:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xdac011c0); // clz x0, x14
  8242:         // p includes the hidden leading bit: 53 for binary64 and 24 for
  8243:         // binary32. Thus shift = (64 + h) - (p - 1) = bias - clz(high).
  8244:         a64_emit_constant_compact(buffer, 2, target_width == 64 ? 75 : 104);
  8245:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xcb000042); // sub x2, x2, x0
  8246:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf101005f); // cmp x2, #64
  8247:         u32 low_branch = (u32)buffer->count;
  8248:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000003); // b.lo low_path
  8249:         u32 shift64_branch = (u32)buffer->count;
  8250:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000000); // b.eq shift64_path
  8251: 
  8252:         // shift > 64: the significand is high >> (shift - 64), the round bit
  8253:         // is immediately below it, and sticky includes the low half plus all
  8254:         // lower high-half bits. x3 is shift - 64 and x4 is the round-bit
  8255:         // position in the high half.
  8256:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xd1010043); // sub x3, x2, #64
  8257:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac325c0); // lsr x0, x14, x3
  8258:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xd1000464); // sub x4, x3, #1
  8259:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac425c1); // lsr x1, x14, x4
  8260:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x92400021); // and x1, x1, #1
  8261:         a64_emit_constant_compact(buffer, 5, 64);
  8262:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xcb0400a5); // sub x5, x5, x4
  8263:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac521c5); // lsl x5, x14, x5
  8264:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf100009f); // cmp x4, #0
  8265:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9a8503e5); // csel x5, xzr, x5, eq
  8266:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf10000bf); // cmp x5, #0
  8267:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9a9f07e4); // cset x4, ne
  8268:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf10001bf); // cmp x13, #0
  8269:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9a9f07e5); // cset x5, ne
  8270:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa050084); // orr x4, x4, x5
  8271:         u32 high_to_round = (u32)buffer->count;
  8272:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x14000000); // b round
  8273: 
  8274:         u32 shift64_offset = (u32)buffer->count;
  8275:         // shift == 64: high is the candidate significand, bit 63 of low is
  8276:         // the round bit, and the remaining low bits are sticky.
  8277:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa0e03e0); // mov x0, x14
  8278:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xd37ffda1); // lsr x1, x13, #63
  8279:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x92400021); // and x1, x1, #1
  8280:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xd37ff9a4); // lsl x4, x13, #1
  8281:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf100009f); // cmp x4, #0
  8282:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9a9f07e4); // cset x4, ne
  8283:         u32 shift64_to_round = (u32)buffer->count;
  8284:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x14000000); // b round
  8285: 
  8286:         u32 low_offset = (u32)buffer->count;
  8287:         // shift < 64: combine the top of high with low >> shift. Sticky is
  8288:         // all low bits below the round bit.
  8289:         a64_emit_constant_compact(buffer, 3, 64);
  8290:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xcb020063); // sub x3, x3, x2
  8291:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac321c0); // lsl x0, x14, x3
  8292:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac225a1); // lsr x1, x13, x2
  8293:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa010000); // orr x0, x0, x1
  8294:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xd1000443); // sub x3, x2, #1
  8295:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac325a1); // lsr x1, x13, x3
  8296:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x92400021); // and x1, x1, #1
  8297:         a64_emit_constant_compact(buffer, 4, 65);
  8298:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xcb020084); // sub x4, x4, x2
  8299:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9ac421a4); // lsl x4, x13, x4
  8300:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf100009f); // cmp x4, #0
  8301:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9a9f07e4); // cset x4, ne
  8302:         u32 low_to_round = (u32)buffer->count;
  8303:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x14000000); // b round
  8304: 
  8305:         u32 round_offset = (u32)buffer->count;
  8306:         // Round to nearest, ties to even. x1 is the round bit, x4 the sticky
  8307:         // flag, and x0's low bit supplies the tie parity.
  8308:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x92400005); // and x5, x0, #1
  8309:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xaa050084); // orr x4, x4, x5
  8310:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x8a010084); // and x4, x4, x1
  8311:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x91000405); // add x5, x0, #1
  8312:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf100009f); // cmp x4, #0
  8313:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9a8010a0); // csel x0, x5, x0, ne
  8314: 
  8315:         u32 convert_offset = (u32)buffer->count;
  8316:         if (target_width == 32)
  8317:         {
  8318:             // x0 has at most 24 significant bits here, so this conversion is
  8319:             // exact. Scale in binary32 directly; narrowing a rounded binary64
  8320:             // value would introduce a second rounding step.
  8321:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9e230000); // ucvtf s0, x0
  8322:             a64_emit_constant(buffer, 16, UINT64_C(0x3f800000)); // float 1.0
  8323:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x8b025e10); // add x16, x16, x2, lsl #23
  8324:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e270201); // fmov s1, w16
  8325:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e210800); // fmul s0, s0, s1
  8326:         }
  8327:         else
  8328:         {
  8329:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9e630000); // ucvtf d0, x0
  8330:             a64_emit_constant(buffer, 16, UINT64_C(0x3ff0000000000000)); // 1.0
  8331:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x8b02d210); // add x16, x16, x2, lsl #52
  8332:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9e670201); // fmov d1, x16
  8333:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e610800); // fmul d0, d0, d1
  8334:         }
  8335: 
  8336:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, direct_branch, general_offset, true);
  8337:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, direct_to_convert, convert_offset, false);
  8338:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, low_branch, low_offset, true);
  8339:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, shift64_branch, shift64_offset, true);
  8340:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, high_to_round, round_offset, false);
  8341:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, shift64_to_round, round_offset, false);
  8342:         codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, low_to_round, round_offset, false);
  8343:         if (target_width == 32)
  8344:         {
  8345:             if (signed_value)
  8346:             {
  8347:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf10001ff); // cmp x15, #0
  8348:                 u32 skip_negate = (u32)buffer->count;
  8349:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000000); // b.eq skip_negate
  8350:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e214000); // fneg s0, s0
  8351:                 u32 after_negate = (u32)buffer->count;
  8352:                 codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, skip_negate, after_negate, true);
  8353:             }
  8354:             result = codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(buffer, 0, result_offset, 4, true);
  8355:         }
  8356:         else
  8357:         {
  8358:             if (signed_value)
  8359:             {
  8360:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xf10001ff); // cmp x15, #0
  8361:                 u32 skip_negate = (u32)buffer->count;
  8362:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x54000000); // b.eq skip_negate
  8363:                 codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e614000); // fneg d0, d0
  8364:                 u32 after_negate = (u32)buffer->count;
  8365:                 codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(buffer, skip_negate, after_negate, true);
  8366:             }
  8367:             result = codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(buffer, 0, result_offset, 8, true);
  8368:         }
  8369:     }
  8370:     else if (buffer && buffer->error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE)
  8371:     {
  8372:         buffer->error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
  8373:     }
  8374:     return result && buffer && buffer->error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE;
  8375: }
  8376: 
  8377: // Converts a scalar float/double in a frame slot to an i128 pair. Values are
  8378: // split at 2^64 so each FCVTZU consumes a representable unsigned 64-bit
  8379: // range; signed inputs use the absolute magnitude and restore the sign in the
  8380: // integer pair. Float inputs are widened to double before the split.
  8381: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool codegen_canonical_a64_float_to_i128(CodegenBuffer* buffer, u32 source_offset, u32 result_offset, u32 source_width,
  8382:                                                             bool signed_value)
  8383: {
  8384:     bool result = false;
  8385:     if (buffer && (source_width == 32 || source_width == 64) &&
  8386:         codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(buffer, 0, source_offset, source_width / 8, false))
  8387:     {
  8388:         if (source_width == 32)
  8389:         {
  8390:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e22c000); // fcvt d0, s0
  8391:         }
  8392:         if (signed_value)
  8393:         {
  8394:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9e66000f); // fmov x15, d0
  8395:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x937ffdef); // asr x15, x15, #63
  8396:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e60c000); // fabs d0, d0
  8397:         }
  8398:         a64_emit_constant(buffer, 16, UINT64_C(0x3bf0000000000000)); // 2^-64
  8399:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9e670201); // fmov d1, x16
  8400:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e610801); // fmul d1, d0, d1
  8401:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9e79002a); // fcvtzu x10, d1
  8402:         a64_emit_constant(buffer, 16, UINT64_C(0x43f0000000000000)); // 2^64
  8403:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9e630141); // ucvtf d1, x10
  8404:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9e670202); // fmov d2, x16
  8405:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e620821); // fmul d1, d1, d2
  8406:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x1e613800); // fsub d0, d0, d1
  8407:         codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0x9e790009); // fcvtzu x9, d0
  8408:         if (signed_value)
  8409:         {
  8410:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca0f0129); // eor x9, x9, x15
  8411:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xca0f014a); // eor x10, x10, x15
  8412:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xeb0f0129); // subs x9, x9, x15
  8413:             codegen_emit_u32(buffer, 0xda0f014a); // sbc x10, x10, x15
  8414:         }
  8415:         result = codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(buffer, 9, result_offset, 8, true, false) &&
  8416:                  codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(buffer, 10, result_offset + 8, 8, true, false);
  8417:     }
  8418:     else if (buffer && buffer->error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE)
  8419:     {
  8420:         buffer->error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
  8421:     }
  8422:     return result && buffer && buffer->error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE;
  8423: }
  8424: 
  8425: // Which data section a global's bytes belong in. `const` is the frontend's
  8426: // answer and the read-only section is its usual home, but an object that
  8427: // carries a relocation cannot stay there: those bytes are written when the
  8428: // program is relocated, and in a shared object that write lands on a page the
  8429: // loader mapped read-only, which is a DT_TEXTREL the loader has to undo or
  8430: // refuse. Clang answers the same question with a fourth data section,
  8431: // `.data.rel.ro`; this writer has three, so a read-only object holding a
  8432: // relocation is laid out with the writable ones. Nothing in the C object model
  8433: // moves with it -- writing through a `const` lvalue is undefined either way --
  8434: // and a static link, whose relocations are all resolved before the program
  8435: // runs, cannot tell the difference.
  8436: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool codegen_global_is_read_only(IrGlobal* global)
  8437: {
  8438:     return global->is_read_only && !global->relocation_count && global->initializer_kind != IR_GLOBAL_INITIALIZER_SYMBOL_ADDRESS;
  8439: }
  8440: 
  8441: // What one canonical value of a type costs the frame, read by the capacity
  8442: // estimate's walk over every value of every function in place of the type
  8443: // record itself: the record is ~152 bytes and the walk wants 13 of them, so
  8444: // a million random reads of it were the walk's whole cost. One row per
  8445: // program type, built once per module beside the f80 cache -- it depends on
  8446: // nothing an attempt produces, so it survives a retry -- and eight rows share
  8447: // a cache line. `size` is the slot rounded up to eightbytes and never under
  8448: // one; `alignment` is the type's, never under eight. A zero `size` marks a
  8449: // type the walk cannot size, an unresolved layout or one past the u32 frame
  8450: // the emitter addresses, which is an error for every value but a global place.
  8451: typedef struct CodegenSlotCost CodegenSlotCost;
  8452: struct CodegenSlotCost
  8453: {
  8454:     u32 size;
  8455:     u32 alignment;
  8456: };
  8457: BUSTER_CT_CHECK(sizeof(CodegenSlotCost) == 8);
  8458: 
  8459: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL CodegenSlotCost* codegen_slot_costs_build(Arena* arena, IrTypeTable const* types)
  8460: {
  8461:     CodegenSlotCost* result = arena_allocate(arena, CodegenSlotCost, types->count ? types->count : 1);
  8462:     for (u32 type_index = 0; type_index < types->count; type_index += 1)
  8463:     {
  8464:         IrTypeLayout layout = types->types[type_index].layout;
  8465:         bool sized = layout.resolved && layout.size <= UINT32_MAX - 7;
  8466:         u64 slot_size = (layout.size + 7) & ~(u64)7;
  8467:         result[type_index] = (CodegenSlotCost){
  8468:             .size = sized ? (u32)BUSTER_MAX(slot_size, 8u) : 0,
  8469:             .alignment = BUSTER_MAX(layout.alignment, 8u),
  8470:         };
  8471:     }
  8472:     return result;
  8473: }
  8474: 
  8475: // The line rows of one machine-emitted function, from the selector's marks --
  8476: // one per lowered instruction, in row order -- and the encoder's row offsets.
  8477: // A position is recovered here rather than carried through selection, so a
  8478: // row that never reaches the line table costs nothing to resolve, and which
  8479: // rows those are is known before any position is: the record helper drops a
  8480: // row at the last recorded offset (a row that emitted no bytes, or one behind
  8481: // a row that did) and drops everything once the table is full, so those marks
  8482: // are not resolved at all -- three marks in ten on the self-host stage. A mark
  8483: // that repeats its predecessor's range, which every instruction of one
  8484: // expression does, takes the position already in hand instead of the program
  8485: // cursor's memo behind two calls. The rows appended, and their order, are
  8486: // exactly those of resolving every mark: a position only ever decides whether
  8487: // a row is appended, never where, and a mark skipped here would have been
  8488: // dropped on its offset alone.
  8489: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL void codegen_record_machine_line_marks(IrProgram* program, IrFunction* ir_function, CodegenModule* result,
  8490:                                                             u32 line_entry_capacity, u32 line_source_limit, MachineFunction const* function,
  8491:                                                             u32 const* row_offsets, u32 code_base)
  8492: {
  8493:     CodegenLineEntry* entries = result->line_entries;
  8494:     if (entries)
  8495:     {
  8496:         // The mark names its IR row; its source range is recovered here
  8497:         // rather than carried through selection, and a row without one is
  8498:         // skipped before the last-entry test so the entry sequence is the
  8499:         // one the ranged-only marks produced. A memo source of
  8500:         // IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID reads as "no position yet".
  8501:         u32 memo_source = IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID;
  8502:         u32 memo_offset = 0;
  8503:         IrSourcePosition memo_position = {0};
  8504:         for (u32 mark_index = 0; mark_index < function->line_mark_count; mark_index += 1)
  8505:         {
  8506:             MachineLineMark mark = function->line_marks[mark_index];
  8507:             IrSourceRange mark_source = ir_instruction_canonical_source(ir_function, (IrInstructionId){.value = mark.instruction});
  8508:             if (mark.row < function->instruction_count && mark_source.source.value != IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID)
  8509:             {
  8510:                 u32 code_offset = code_base + row_offsets[mark.row];
  8511:                 u32 count = result->line_entry_count;
  8512:                 bool dropped = count >= line_entry_capacity || (count && entries[count - 1].code_offset == code_offset);
  8513:                 if (!dropped)
  8514:                 {
  8515:                     if (mark_source.source.value != memo_source || mark_source.offset != memo_offset)
  8516:                     {
  8517:                         memo_position = ir_source_position(program, mark_source);
  8518:                         memo_source = mark_source.source.value;
  8519:                         memo_offset = mark_source.offset;
  8520:                     }
  8521:                     codegen_record_line_hot(entries, &result->line_entry_count, line_entry_capacity, code_offset, mark_source.source.value,
  8522:                                             line_source_limit, memo_position.line, memo_position.column);
  8523:                 }
  8524:             }
  8525:         }
  8526:     }
  8527: }
  8528: 
  8529: // One generation of the whole module -- globals, functions and global assembly
  8530: // -- into a code buffer reserved at `capacity_scale` times the flat estimate
  8531: // below. Everything it produces comes out of `arena`, so a caller that does not
  8532: // like the answer can rewind and ask again; the target, the program ABI and the
  8533: // IR validation are its caller's business and are not repeated per attempt.
  8534: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL CodegenModule codegen_generate_canonical_module_attempt(Arena* arena, IrProgram* program,
  8535:                                                                            CodegenCanonicalX64F80Cache const* f80_cache,
  8536:                                                                            CodegenSlotCost const* slot_costs, IrModule* module,
  8537:                                                                            Target target, CodegenModuleOptions options, u64 capacity_scale,
  8538:                                                                            bool* code_buffer_exhausted,
  8539:                                                                            CodegenX64MetadataCache* x64_metadata_cache,
  8540:                                                                            MachineSelectionModule* machine_module)
  8541: {
  8542:     CodegenModule result = {
  8543:         .ir_module = module,
  8544:         .abi = codegen_abi_for_target(target),
  8545:     };
  8546:     // The one place -fPIC is turned into a fact about this module. It is a
  8547:     // statement about which references `ld` will place in a shared object, so
  8548:     // it is scoped to the format and architecture whose relocations say that:
  8549:     // x86-64 ELF. Windows images relocate as a whole and Mach-O's model is
  8550:     // its own; neither reads this flag.
  8551:     bool position_independent =
  8552:         options.position_independent && target.cpu_arch == CPU_ARCH_X86_64 && object_format_for_target(target) == OBJECT_FORMAT_ELF64;
  8553:     result.position_independent = position_independent;
  8554:     result.globals = arena_allocate(arena, CodegenModuleGlobal, module->global_count);
  8555:     u64 read_only_capacity = 0;
  8556:     u64 writable_capacity = 0;
  8557:     u64 thread_local_capacity = 0;
  8558:     u64 zero_fill_capacity = 0;
  8559:     u64 thread_local_zero_capacity = 0;
  8560:     for (u32 global_index = 0; global_index < module->global_count; global_index += 1)
  8561:     {
 17483:                             }
 17484:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < aggregate_parts; part_index += 1)
 17485:                             {
 17486:                                 u32 part_offset = part_index * 8;
 17487:                                 u64 part_size = BUSTER_MIN((u64)8, loaded_type->layout.size - part_offset);
 17488:                                 u64 part_copied = 0;
 17489:                                 while (part_copied < part_size)
 17490:                                 {
 17491:                                     u32 copy_offset = part_offset + (u32)part_copied;
 17492:                                     u64 remaining = part_size - part_copied;
 17493:                                     u32 chunk = codegen_canonical_copy_chunk(
 17494:                                         remaining, indirect ? copy_offset : value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value] + copy_offset,
 17495:                                         result_offset + copy_offset);
 17496:                                     if (indirect)
 17497:                                     {
 17498:                                         a64_emit_load_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 10, copy_offset, chunk);
 17499:                                     }
 17500:                                     else
 17501:                                     {
 17502:                                         a64_emit_load_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value] + copy_offset, chunk);
 17503:                                     }
 17504:                                     a64_emit_store_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, result_offset + copy_offset, chunk);
 17505:                                     part_copied += chunk;
 17506:                                 }
 17507:                             }
 17508:                         }
 17509:                         else if (indirect)
 17510:                         {
 17511:                             if (!loaded_type || !loaded_type->layout.resolved ||
 17512:                                 (loaded_type->layout.size != 1 && loaded_type->layout.size != 2 && loaded_type->layout.size != 4 &&
 17513:                                  loaded_type->layout.size != 8))
 17514:                             {
 17515:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 17516:                                 return result;
 17517:                             }
 17518:                             a64_emit_load_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value], 8);
 17519:                             a64_emit_load_pointer(&buffer, 9, 9, (u32)loaded_type->layout.size);
 17520:                         }
 17521:                         else
 17522:                         {
 17523:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
 17524:                         }
 17525:                         if (!aggregate)
 17526:                         {
 17527:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17528:                         }
 17529:                     }
 17530:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX)
 17531:                     {
 17532:                         IrValueId base = instruction->operands[0];

 11540:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand extend_operands[2] = {
 11541:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, wide_unsigned && wide_integer_width < 64 ? 32 : 64),
 11542:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)(wide_integer_width == 32 && wide_unsigned ? 32 : wide_integer_width)),
 11543:                                 };
 11544:                                 if (wide_integer_width != 64)
 11545:                                 {
 11546:                                     extend_mnemonic = wide_unsigned ? (wide_integer_width == 32 ? S8("MOV") : S8("MOVZX"))
 11547:                                                                     : (wide_integer_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSXD") : S8("MOVSX"));
 11548:                                 }
 11549:                                 wide_emitted = (!extend_mnemonic.length ||
 11550:                                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, extend_mnemonic, extend_operands,
 11551:                                                                                     BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(extend_operands))) &&
 11552:                                                codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_from_integer(&buffer, wide_unsigned && wide_integer_width == 64,
 11553:                                                                                            wide_scratch_displacement, result_displacement,
 11554:                                                                                            &x87_stack_depth);
 11555:                             }
 11556:                             else if (wide_source_scalar && wide_integer_end &&
 11557:                                      (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER ||
 11558:                                       conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER))
 11559:                             {
 11560:                                 // Narrower C destinations keep the eightbyte
 11561:                                 // conversion and let the frame store narrow,
 11562:                                 // exactly as the SSE path does; only an
 11563:                                 // unsigned eightbyte destination needs the
 11564:                                 // bias, because FISTP writes a signed image.
 11565:                                 bool wide_unsigned_64 = conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER && wide_integer_width == 64;
 11566:                                 wide_emitted = wide_unsigned_64
 11567:                                                    ? codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_to_unsigned_64(&buffer, wide_source_displacement,
 11568:                                                                                                    wide_scratch_displacement, &x87_stack_depth)
 11569:                                                    : codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_to_integer(&buffer, wide_source_displacement,
 11570:                                                                                                wide_scratch_displacement, &x87_stack_depth);
 11571:                                 if (wide_emitted)
 11572:                                 {
 11573:                                     c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11574:                                     wide_emitted = buffer.error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE;
 11575:                                 }
 11576:                             }
 11577:                             if (!wide_emitted)
 11578:                             {
 11579:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11580:                                 return result;
 11581:                             }
 11582:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 11583:                             continue;
 11584:                         }
 11585:                         if (source_contains_f80 || target_contains_f80)
 11586:                         {
 11587:                             if (conversion != IR_CONVERSION_IDENTITY || !source_contains_f80 || !target_contains_f80 ||
 11588:                                 result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V || !source_type->layout.resolved || !target_type->layout.resolved ||
 11589:                                 source_type->layout.size != 16 || target_type->layout.size != 16 ||
 11590:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_x87_shape_cached(
 11591:                                     f80_cache, program, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type) ||
 11592:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_x87_shape_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type) ||
 11593:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_copy(
 11594:                                     &buffer, X64_REGISTER_RBP,
 11595:                                     c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]), X64_REGISTER_RBP,
 11596:                                     result_displacement, &x87_stack_depth))
 11597:                             {
 11598:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11599:                                 return result;
 11600:                             }
 11601:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 11602:                             continue;
 11603:                         }
 11604:                         bool source_integer128 = source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && source_type->bit_width == 128;
 11605:                         bool target_integer128 = target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && target_type->bit_width == 128;
 11606:                         if (source_integer128 || target_integer128)
 11607:                         {
 11608:                             if (source_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER || target_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
 11609:                             {
 11610:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11611:                                 return result;
 11612:                             }
 11613:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
 11614:                             if (target_integer128)
 11615:                             {
 11616:                                 if (source_integer128)
 11617:                                 {
 11618:                                     c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[0]);
 11619:                                 }
 11620:                                 else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND)
 11621:                                 {
 11622:                                     String8 extend_mnemonic = {0};
 11623:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand extend_operands[2];
 11624:                                     u32 extend_operand_count = 0;
 11625:                                     if (source_type->bit_width == 8 || source_type->bit_width == 16)
 11626:                                     {
 11627:                                         extend_mnemonic = S8("MOVSX");
 11628:                                         extend_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
 11629:                                         extend_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_type->bit_width);
 11630:                                         extend_operand_count = 2;
 11631:                                     }
 11632:                                     else if (source_type->bit_width == 32)
 11633:                                     {
 11634:                                         extend_mnemonic = S8("MOVSXD");
 11635:                                         extend_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
 11636:                                         extend_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11637:                                         extend_operand_count = 2;
 11638:                                     }
 11639:                                     if ((extend_operand_count && !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, extend_mnemonic, extend_operands, extend_operand_count)) ||
 11640:                                         !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("CQO"), 0, 0))
 11641:                                     {
 11642:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
 11643:                                         return result;
 11644:                                     }
 11645:                                 }
 11646:                                 else
 11647:                                 {
 11648:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand zero_extend_operands[2];
 11649:                                     u32 zero_extend_operand_count = 0;
 11650:                                     if (source_type->bit_width <= 32)
 11651:                                     {
 11652:                                         if (source_type->bit_width < 32)
 11653:                                         {
 11654:                                             zero_extend_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11655:                                             zero_extend_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_type->bit_width);
 11656:                                             zero_extend_operand_count = 2;
 11657:                                         }
 11658:                                         else
 11659:                                         {
 11660:                                             zero_extend_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11661:                                             zero_extend_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11662:                                             zero_extend_operand_count = 2;
 11663:                                         }
 11664:                                     }
 11665:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand clear_high_operands[2] = {
 11666:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
 11667:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
 11668:                                     };
 11669:                                     String8 zero_extend_mnemonic = source_type->bit_width < 32 ? S8("MOVZX") : S8("MOV");
 11670:                                     if ((zero_extend_operand_count && !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, zero_extend_mnemonic, zero_extend_operands,
 11671:                                                                                                              zero_extend_operand_count)) ||
 11672:                                         !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("XOR"), clear_high_operands, 2))
 11673:                                     {
 11674:                                         result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11675:                                         return result;
 11676:                                     }
 11677:                                 }
 11678:                                 c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11679:                                 c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11680:                             }
 11681:                             else
 11682:                             {
 11683:                                 if (target_type->bit_width != 8 && target_type->bit_width != 16 && target_type->bit_width != 32 &&
 11684:                                     target_type->bit_width != 64)
 11685:                                 {
 11686:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11687:                                     return result;
 11688:                                 }
 11689:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand truncate_operands[2];
 11690:                                 u32 truncate_operand_count = 0;
 11691:                                 if (target_type->bit_width == 8 || target_type->bit_width == 16)
 11692:                                 {
 11693:                                     truncate_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11694:                                     truncate_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)target_type->bit_width);
 11695:                                     truncate_operand_count = 2;
 11696:                                 }
 11697:                                 else if (target_type->bit_width == 32)
 11698:                                 {
 11699:                                     truncate_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11700:                                     truncate_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11701:                                     truncate_operand_count = 2;
 11702:                                 }
 11703:                                 // MOVZX has no 32-to-32 form; the plain MOV
 11704:                                 // already zeroes the upper doubleword.
 11705:                                 if (truncate_operand_count &&
 11706:                                     !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, target_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOV") : S8("MOVZX"),
 11707:                                                                           truncate_operands, truncate_operand_count))
 11708:                                 {
 11709:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
 11710:                                     return result;
 11711:                                 }
 11712:                                 c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11713:                             }
 11714:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 11715:                             continue;
 11716:                         }
 11717:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
 11718:                         if (buffer.error)
 11719:                         {
 11720:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 11721:                             return result;
 11722:                         }
 11723:                         bool source_integer = source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER || source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ||
 11724:                                               source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER;
 11725:                         bool target_integer = target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER || target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ||
 11726:                                               target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER;
 11727:                         if (source_integer && target_integer &&
 11728:                             (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_ZERO_EXTEND ||
 11729:                              conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_TRUNCATE || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_REINTERPRET ||
 11730:                              conversion == IR_CONVERSION_POINTER_TO_INTEGER || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_TO_POINTER ||
 11731:                              conversion == IR_CONVERSION_POINTER_REINTERPRET || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_IDENTITY))
 11732:                         {
 11733:                             u32 source_bit_width = source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ? 8
 11734:                                                  : source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER ? 64
 11735:                                                                                          : source_type->bit_width;
 11736:                             u32 target_bit_width = target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ? 8
 11737:                                                  : target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER ? 64
 11738:                                                                                          : target_type->bit_width;
 11739:                             if ((source_bit_width != 8 && source_bit_width != 16 && source_bit_width != 32 && source_bit_width != 64) ||
 11740:                                 (target_bit_width != 8 && target_bit_width != 16 && target_bit_width != 32 && target_bit_width != 64))
 11741:                             {
 11742:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11743:                                 return result;
 11744:                             }
 11745:                             String8 mnemonic = {0};
 11746:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand cast_operands[2];
 11747:                             u32 cast_operand_count = 2;
 11748:                             if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND)
 11749:                             {
 11750:                                 if (source_bit_width == 64)
 11751:                                 {
 11752:                                     // A 64-bit source is already in the canonical
 11753:                                     // register width; no instruction is required.
 11754:                                     cast_operand_count = 0;
 11755:                                 }
 11756:                                 else
 11757:                                 {
 11758:                                     mnemonic = source_bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSXD") : S8("MOVSX");
 11759:                                     cast_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
 11760:                                     cast_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_bit_width);
 11761:                                 }
 11762:                             }
 11763:                             else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_ZERO_EXTEND)
 11764:                             {
 11765:                                 if (source_bit_width == 64)
 11766:                                 {
 11767:                                     cast_operand_count = 0;
 11768:                                 }
 11769:                                 else if (source_bit_width == 32)
 11770:                                 {
 11771:                                     mnemonic = S8("MOV");
 11772:                                     cast_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11773:                                     cast_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11774:                                 }
 11775:                                 else
 11776:                                 {
 11777:                                     mnemonic = S8("MOVZX");
 11778:                                     cast_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11779:                                     cast_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_bit_width);
 11780:                                 }
 11781:                             }
 11782:                             else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_TRUNCATE)
 11783:                             {
 11784:                                 if (target_bit_width == 64)
 11426:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11427:                     }
 11428:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE)
 11429:                     {
 11430:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
 11431:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11432:                     }
 11433:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD)
 11434:                     {
 11435:                         IrValueId base = instruction->operands[0];
 11436:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + function->values[base.value].definition.value;
 11437:                         if (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL)
 11438:                         {
 11439:                             if (function->values[base.value].alignment > 16)
 11440:                             {
 11441:                                 c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, base);
 11442:                             }
 11443:                             else
 11444:                             {
 11445:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand field_address_operands[2] = {
 11446:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
 11447:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
 11448:                                         X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[base.value])),
 11449:                                 };
 11450:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("LEA"), field_address_operands,
 11451:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(field_address_operands)))
 11452:                                 {
 11453:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
 11454:                                     return result;
 11455:                                 }
 11456:                             }
 11457:                         }
 11458:                         else
 11459:                         {
 11460:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, base);
 11461:                         }
 11462:                         IrType* aggregate = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[base.value].canonical_type);
 11463:                         u64 field_index = instruction->immediates[0];
 11464:                         if (!aggregate || field_index >= aggregate->field_count || aggregate->fields[field_index].offset > INT32_MAX)
 11465:                         {
 11466:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 11467:                             return result;
 11468:                         }
 11469:                         // The first field of an aggregate sits at offset zero,
 11470:                         // so the address arithmetic is the identity. Only the
 11471:                         // result store follows, and it does not read flags.
 11472:                         if (aggregate->fields[field_index].offset)
 11473:                         {
 11474:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand field_add_operands[2] = {
 11475:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
 11476:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate((s64)aggregate->fields[field_index].offset, 32),
 11477:                             };
 11478:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), field_add_operands,
 11479:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(field_add_operands)))
 11480:                             {
 11481:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 11482:                                 return result;
 11483:                             }
 11484:                         }
 11485:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11486:                     }
 11487:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CAST)
 11488:                     {
 11489:                         IrType* source_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
 11490:                         IrType* target_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 11491:                         IrConversionOperation conversion = instruction->conversion_operation;
 11492:                         if (!target_type || !source_type)
 11493:                         {
 11494:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 11495:                             return result;
 11496:                         }
 11497:                         bool source_contains_f80 = codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(
 11498:                             f80_cache, program, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
 11499:                         bool target_contains_f80 = codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type);
 11500:                         // A conversion with exactly one scalar f80 end runs on
 11501:                         // the x87 stack.  FLD/FSTP change format on their own,
 11502:                         // so the float directions are one push and one pop;
 11503:                         // the integer directions go through FILD/FISTP and the
 11504:                         // scratch area.
 11505:                         bool wide_source_scalar = canonical_function_has_f80 && codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80(source_type);
 11506:                         bool wide_target_scalar = canonical_function_has_f80 && codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80(target_type);
 11507:                         if ((wide_source_scalar || wide_target_scalar) && conversion != IR_CONVERSION_IDENTITY)
 11508:                         {
 11509:                             s32 wide_source_displacement = c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
 11510:                             s32 wide_scratch_displacement = codegen_canonical_x64_rebase_frame_displacement(
 11511:                                 &buffer, canonical_x87_scratch_displacement, canonical_x64_frame_base_offset);
 11512:                             u32 wide_integer_width = wide_target_scalar ? source_type->bit_width : target_type->bit_width;
 11513:                             bool wide_integer_end = (wide_target_scalar ? source_type : target_type)->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
 11514:                                                     (wide_integer_width == 8 || wide_integer_width == 16 || wide_integer_width == 32 ||
 11515:                                                      wide_integer_width == 64);
 11516:                             // Both ends f80 is the identity below, never a
 11517:                             // conversion opcode, so it is not an arm here.
 11518:                             bool wide_emitted = false;
 11519:                             if (!wide_source_scalar && conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_EXTEND && wide_target_scalar &&
 11520:                                 source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && (source_type->bit_width == 32 || source_type->bit_width == 64))
 11521:                             {
 11522:                                 wide_emitted = codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_float_convert(&buffer, (u16)source_type->bit_width, 80,
 11523:                                                                                             wide_source_displacement, result_displacement,
 11524:                                                                                             &x87_stack_depth);
 11525:                             }
 11526:                             else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TRUNCATE && wide_source_scalar && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
 11527:                                      (target_type->bit_width == 32 || target_type->bit_width == 64))
 11528:                             {
 11529:                                 wide_emitted = codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_float_convert(&buffer, 80, (u16)target_type->bit_width,
 11530:                                                                                             wide_source_displacement, result_displacement,
 11531:                                                                                             &x87_stack_depth);
 11532:                             }
 11533:                             else if (wide_target_scalar && wide_integer_end &&
 11534:                                      (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT ||
 11535:                                       conversion == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT))
 11536:                             {
 11537:                                 bool wide_unsigned = conversion == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT;
 11538:                                 c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
 11539:                                 String8 extend_mnemonic = {0};
 11540:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand extend_operands[2] = {
 11541:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, wide_unsigned && wide_integer_width < 64 ? 32 : 64),
 11542:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)(wide_integer_width == 32 && wide_unsigned ? 32 : wide_integer_width)),
 11543:                                 };
 11544:                                 if (wide_integer_width != 64)
 11545:                                 {
 11546:                                     extend_mnemonic = wide_unsigned ? (wide_integer_width == 32 ? S8("MOV") : S8("MOVZX"))
 11547:                                                                     : (wide_integer_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSXD") : S8("MOVSX"));
 11548:                                 }
 11549:                                 wide_emitted = (!extend_mnemonic.length ||
 11550:                                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, extend_mnemonic, extend_operands,
 11551:                                                                                     BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(extend_operands))) &&
 11552:                                                codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_from_integer(&buffer, wide_unsigned && wide_integer_width == 64,
 11553:                                                                                            wide_scratch_displacement, result_displacement,
 11554:                                                                                            &x87_stack_depth);
 11555:                             }
 11556:                             else if (wide_source_scalar && wide_integer_end &&
 11557:                                      (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER ||
 11558:                                       conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER))
 11559:                             {
 11560:                                 // Narrower C destinations keep the eightbyte
 11561:                                 // conversion and let the frame store narrow,
 11562:                                 // exactly as the SSE path does; only an
 11563:                                 // unsigned eightbyte destination needs the
 11564:                                 // bias, because FISTP writes a signed image.
 11565:                                 bool wide_unsigned_64 = conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER && wide_integer_width == 64;
 11566:                                 wide_emitted = wide_unsigned_64
 11567:                                                    ? codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_to_unsigned_64(&buffer, wide_source_displacement,
 11568:                                                                                                    wide_scratch_displacement, &x87_stack_depth)
 11569:                                                    : codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_to_integer(&buffer, wide_source_displacement,
 11570:                                                                                                wide_scratch_displacement, &x87_stack_depth);
 11571:                                 if (wide_emitted)
 11572:                                 {
 11573:                                     c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11574:                                     wide_emitted = buffer.error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE;
 11575:                                 }
 11576:                             }
 11577:                             if (!wide_emitted)
 11578:                             {
 11579:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11580:                                 return result;
 11581:                             }
 11582:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 11583:                             continue;
 11584:                         }
 11585:                         if (source_contains_f80 || target_contains_f80)
 11586:                         {
 11587:                             if (conversion != IR_CONVERSION_IDENTITY || !source_contains_f80 || !target_contains_f80 ||
 11588:                                 result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V || !source_type->layout.resolved || !target_type->layout.resolved ||
 11589:                                 source_type->layout.size != 16 || target_type->layout.size != 16 ||
 11590:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_x87_shape_cached(

 11592:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_x87_shape_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type) ||
