 11596:                                     result_displacement, &x87_stack_depth))
 11597:                             {
 11598:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11599:                                 return result;
 11600:                             }
 11601:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 11602:                             continue;
 11603:                         }
 11604:                         bool source_integer128 = source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && source_type->bit_width == 128;
 11605:                         bool target_integer128 = target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && target_type->bit_width == 128;
 11606:                         if (source_integer128 || target_integer128)
 11607:                         {
 11608:                             if (source_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER || target_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
 11609:                             {
 11610:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11611:                                 return result;
 11612:                             }
 11613:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
 11614:                             if (target_integer128)
 11615:                             {
 11616:                                 if (source_integer128)
 11617:                                 {
 11618:                                     c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[0]);
 11619:                                 }
 11620:                                 else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND)
 11621:                                 {
 11622:                                     String8 extend_mnemonic = {0};
 11623:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand extend_operands[2];
 11624:                                     u32 extend_operand_count = 0;
 11625:                                     if (source_type->bit_width == 8 || source_type->bit_width == 16)
 11626:                                     {
 11627:                                         extend_mnemonic = S8("MOVSX");
 11628:                                         extend_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
 11629:                                         extend_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_type->bit_width);
 11630:                                         extend_operand_count = 2;
 11631:                                     }
 11632:                                     else if (source_type->bit_width == 32)
 11633:                                     {
 11634:                                         extend_mnemonic = S8("MOVSXD");
 11635:                                         extend_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
 11636:                                         extend_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11637:                                         extend_operand_count = 2;
 11638:                                     }
 11639:                                     if ((extend_operand_count && !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, extend_mnemonic, extend_operands, extend_operand_count)) ||
 11640:                                         !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("CQO"), 0, 0))
 11641:                                     {
 11642:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
 11643:                                         return result;
 11644:                                     }
 11645:                                 }
 11646:                                 else
 11647:                                 {
 11648:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand zero_extend_operands[2];
 11649:                                     u32 zero_extend_operand_count = 0;
 11650:                                     if (source_type->bit_width <= 32)
 11651:                                     {
 11652:                                         if (source_type->bit_width < 32)
 11653:                                         {
 11654:                                             zero_extend_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11655:                                             zero_extend_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_type->bit_width);
 11656:                                             zero_extend_operand_count = 2;
 11657:                                         }
 11658:                                         else
 11659:                                         {
 11660:                                             zero_extend_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11661:                                             zero_extend_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11662:                                             zero_extend_operand_count = 2;
 11663:                                         }
 11664:                                     }

 11666:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
 11667:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
 11668:                                     };

 11670:                                     if ((zero_extend_operand_count && !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, zero_extend_mnemonic, zero_extend_operands,
 11671:                                                                                                              zero_extend_operand_count)) ||

 11673:                                     {

 11675:                                         return result;
 11676:                                     }
 11677:                                 }
 11678:                                 c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11679:                                 c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11680:                             }
 11681:                             else
 11682:                             {
 11683:                                 if (target_type->bit_width != 8 && target_type->bit_width != 16 && target_type->bit_width != 32 &&

 11685:                                 {
 11686:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11687:                                     return result;
 11688:                                 }

 11690:                                 u32 truncate_operand_count = 0;

 11692:                                 {

 11696:                                 }
 11697:                                 else if (target_type->bit_width == 32)
 11698:                                 {
 11699:                                     truncate_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11700:                                     truncate_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11701:                                     truncate_operand_count = 2;
 11702:                                 }

 11704:                                 // already zeroes the upper doubleword.

 11708:                                 {
 11709:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
 11710:                                     return result;
 11711:                                 }
 11712:                                 c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11713:                             }
 11714:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 11715:                             continue;
 11716:                         }
 11717:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
 11718:                         if (buffer.error)
 11719:                         {
 11720:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 11721:                             return result;
 11722:                         }
 11723:                         bool source_integer = source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER || source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ||
 11724:                                               source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER;
 11725:                         bool target_integer = target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER || target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ||
 11726:                                               target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER;
 11727:                         if (source_integer && target_integer &&
 11728:                             (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_ZERO_EXTEND ||

 11741:                             {
 11742:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11743:                                 return result;
 11744:                             }
 11745:                             String8 mnemonic = {0};
 11746:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand cast_operands[2];
 11747:                             u32 cast_operand_count = 2;
 11748:                             if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND)
 11749:                             {

 11751:                                 {

 11760:                                     cast_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_bit_width);
 11761:                                 }
 11762:                             }
 11763:                             else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_ZERO_EXTEND)
 11764:                             {

 11768:                                 }
 11769:                                 else if (source_bit_width == 32)
 11770:                                 {
 11771:                                     mnemonic = S8("MOV");
 11772:                                     cast_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11773:                                     cast_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11774:                                 }
 11775:                                 else
 11776:                                 {
 11777:                                     mnemonic = S8("MOVZX");
 11778:                                     cast_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11779:                                     cast_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_bit_width);
 11780:                                 }
 11781:                             }
 11782:                             else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_TRUNCATE)
 11783:                             {
 11784:                                 if (target_bit_width == 64)
 11785:                                 {
 11786:                                     cast_operand_count = 0;
 11436:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + function->values[base.value].definition.value;
 11437:                         if (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL)
 11438:                         {
 11439:                             if (function->values[base.value].alignment > 16)
 11440:                             {
 11441:                                 c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, base);
 11442:                             }

 11446:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),

 11457:                         }

 11464:                         if (!aggregate || field_index >= aggregate->field_count || aggregate->fields[field_index].offset > INT32_MAX)
 11465:                         {
 11466:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 11467:                             return result;
 11468:                         }

 11470:                         // so the address arithmetic is the identity. Only the

 11475:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),

 11479:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(field_add_operands)))
 11480:                             {
 11481:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 11482:                                 return result;
 11483:                             }
 11484:                         }
 11485:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11486:                     }
 11487:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CAST)
 11488:                     {
 11489:                         IrType* source_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
 11490:                         IrType* target_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 11491:                         IrConversionOperation conversion = instruction->conversion_operation;
 11492:                         if (!target_type || !source_type)
 11493:                         {
 11494:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 11495:                             return result;
 11496:                         }
 11497:                         bool source_contains_f80 = codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(
 11498:                             f80_cache, program, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
 11499:                         bool target_contains_f80 = codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type);
 11500:                         // A conversion with exactly one scalar f80 end runs on
 11501:                         // the x87 stack.  FLD/FSTP change format on their own,
 11502:                         // so the float directions are one push and one pop;
 11503:                         // the integer directions go through FILD/FISTP and the
 11504:                         // scratch area.
 11505:                         bool wide_source_scalar = canonical_function_has_f80 && codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80(source_type);
 11506:                         bool wide_target_scalar = canonical_function_has_f80 && codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80(target_type);
 11507:                         if ((wide_source_scalar || wide_target_scalar) && conversion != IR_CONVERSION_IDENTITY)
 11508:                         {
 11509:                             s32 wide_source_displacement = c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
 11510:                             s32 wide_scratch_displacement = codegen_canonical_x64_rebase_frame_displacement(
 11511:                                 &buffer, canonical_x87_scratch_displacement, canonical_x64_frame_base_offset);
 11512:                             u32 wide_integer_width = wide_target_scalar ? source_type->bit_width : target_type->bit_width;
 11513:                             bool wide_integer_end = (wide_target_scalar ? source_type : target_type)->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
 11514:                                                     (wide_integer_width == 8 || wide_integer_width == 16 || wide_integer_width == 32 ||
 11515:                                                      wide_integer_width == 64);

 11518:                             bool wide_emitted = false;

 11523:                                                                                             wide_source_displacement, result_displacement,

 11529:                                 wide_emitted = codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_float_convert(&buffer, 80, (u16)target_type->bit_width,
 11530:                                                                                             wide_source_displacement, result_displacement,

 11575:                                 }
 11576:                             }

 11578:                             {
 11579:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11580:                                 return result;
 11581:                             }
 11582:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 11583:                             continue;
 11584:                         }
 11585:                         if (source_contains_f80 || target_contains_f80)

 11611:                                 return result;

 11613:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
 11614:                             if (target_integer128)

 11619:                                 }
 11620:                                 else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND)

 11627:                                         extend_mnemonic = S8("MOVSX");
 11628:                                         extend_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
 11629:                                         extend_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_type->bit_width);
 11630:                                         extend_operand_count = 2;
 11631:                                     }
 11632:                                     else if (source_type->bit_width == 32)
 11633:                                     {
 11634:                                         extend_mnemonic = S8("MOVSXD");
 11635:                                         extend_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
 11636:                                         extend_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11637:                                         extend_operand_count = 2;
 11638:                                     }
 11639:                                     if ((extend_operand_count && !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, extend_mnemonic, extend_operands, extend_operand_count)) ||
 11640:                                         !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("CQO"), 0, 0))
 11641:                                     {
 11642:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
 11643:                                         return result;
 11644:                                     }
 11645:                                 }
 11646:                                 else
 11647:                                 {
 11648:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand zero_extend_operands[2];
 11649:                                     u32 zero_extend_operand_count = 0;
 11650:                                     if (source_type->bit_width <= 32)
 11651:                                     {
 11652:                                         if (source_type->bit_width < 32)
 11653:                                         {
 11654:                                             zero_extend_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11655:                                             zero_extend_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_type->bit_width);
 11656:                                             zero_extend_operand_count = 2;
 11657:                                         }
 11658:                                         else
 11659:                                         {
 11660:                                             zero_extend_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11661:                                             zero_extend_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11662:                                             zero_extend_operand_count = 2;
 11663:                                         }
 11664:                                     }
 11665:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand clear_high_operands[2] = {
 11666:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
 11667:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
 11668:                                     };
 11669:                                     String8 zero_extend_mnemonic = source_type->bit_width < 32 ? S8("MOVZX") : S8("MOV");
 11670:                                     if ((zero_extend_operand_count && !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, zero_extend_mnemonic, zero_extend_operands,
 11671:                                                                                                              zero_extend_operand_count)) ||
 11672:                                         !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("XOR"), clear_high_operands, 2))
 11673:                                     {
 11674:                                         result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11675:                                         return result;
 11676:                                     }
 11677:                                 }
 11678:                                 c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11679:                                 c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11680:                             }
 11681:                             else
 11682:                             {
 11683:                                 if (target_type->bit_width != 8 && target_type->bit_width != 16 && target_type->bit_width != 32 &&
 11684:                                     target_type->bit_width != 64)
 11685:                                 {
 11686:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11687:                                     return result;
 11688:                                 }
 11689:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand truncate_operands[2];
 11690:                                 u32 truncate_operand_count = 0;
 11691:                                 if (target_type->bit_width == 8 || target_type->bit_width == 16)
 11692:                                 {
 11693:                                     truncate_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11694:                                     truncate_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)target_type->bit_width);
 11695:                                     truncate_operand_count = 2;
 11696:                                 }
 11697:                                 else if (target_type->bit_width == 32)
 11698:                                 {
 11699:                                     truncate_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11700:                                     truncate_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11701:                                     truncate_operand_count = 2;
 11702:                                 }
 11703:                                 // MOVZX has no 32-to-32 form; the plain MOV
 11704:                                 // already zeroes the upper doubleword.
 11705:                                 if (truncate_operand_count &&
 11706:                                     !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, target_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOV") : S8("MOVZX"),
 11707:                                                                           truncate_operands, truncate_operand_count))
 11708:                                 {
 11709:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
 11710:                                     return result;
 11711:                                 }
 11712:                                 c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11713:                             }
 11714:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 11715:                             continue;
 11716:                         }
 11717:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
 11718:                         if (buffer.error)
 11719:                         {
 11720:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 11721:                             return result;
 11722:                         }
 11723:                         bool source_integer = source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER || source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ||
 11724:                                               source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER;
 11725:                         bool target_integer = target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER || target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ||
 11726:                                               target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER;
 11727:                         if (source_integer && target_integer &&
 11728:                             (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_ZERO_EXTEND ||
 11729:                              conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_TRUNCATE || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_REINTERPRET ||
 11730:                              conversion == IR_CONVERSION_POINTER_TO_INTEGER || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_TO_POINTER ||
 11731:                              conversion == IR_CONVERSION_POINTER_REINTERPRET || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_IDENTITY))
 11732:                         {
 11733:                             u32 source_bit_width = source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ? 8
 11734:                                                  : source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER ? 64
 11735:                                                                                          : source_type->bit_width;
 11736:                             u32 target_bit_width = target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ? 8
 11737:                                                  : target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER ? 64
 11738:                                                                                          : target_type->bit_width;
 11739:                             if ((source_bit_width != 8 && source_bit_width != 16 && source_bit_width != 32 && source_bit_width != 64) ||
 11740:                                 (target_bit_width != 8 && target_bit_width != 16 && target_bit_width != 32 && target_bit_width != 64))
 11741:                             {
 11742:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11743:                                 return result;
 11744:                             }
 11745:                             String8 mnemonic = {0};
 11746:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand cast_operands[2];
 11747:                             u32 cast_operand_count = 2;
 11748:                             if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND)
 11749:                             {
 11750:                                 if (source_bit_width == 64)
 11751:                                 {
 11752:                                     // A 64-bit source is already in the canonical
 11753:                                     // register width; no instruction is required.
 11754:                                     cast_operand_count = 0;
 11755:                                 }
 11756:                                 else
 11757:                                 {
 11758:                                     mnemonic = source_bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSXD") : S8("MOVSX");
 11759:                                     cast_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
 11760:                                     cast_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_bit_width);
 11761:                                 }
 11762:                             }
 11763:                             else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_ZERO_EXTEND)
 11764:                             {
 11765:                                 if (source_bit_width == 64)
 11766:                                 {
 11767:                                     cast_operand_count = 0;
 11768:                                 }
 11769:                                 else if (source_bit_width == 32)
 11770:                                 {
 11771:                                     mnemonic = S8("MOV");
 11772:                                     cast_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11773:                                     cast_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11774:                                 }
 11775:                                 else
 11776:                                 {
 11777:                                     mnemonic = S8("MOVZX");
 11778:                                     cast_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11779:                                     cast_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_bit_width);
 11780:                                 }
 11781:                             }
 11782:                             else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_TRUNCATE)
 11783:                             {
 11784:                                 if (target_bit_width == 64)
 11785:                                 {
 11786:                                     cast_operand_count = 0;
 11787:                                 }
 11788:                                 else if (target_bit_width == 32)
 11789:                                 {
 11790:                                     mnemonic = S8("MOV");
 11791:                                     cast_operands[0] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11792:                                     cast_operands[1] = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32);
 11793:                                 }
 11794:                                 else
 11795:                                 {
 11796:                                     mnemonic = S8("MOVZX");
 17478:                                 return result;
 17479:                             }
 17480:                             if (indirect)
 17481:                             {
 17482:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
 17483:                             }
 17484:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < aggregate_parts; part_index += 1)
 17485:                             {
 17486:                                 u32 part_offset = part_index * 8;
 17487:                                 u64 part_size = BUSTER_MIN((u64)8, loaded_type->layout.size - part_offset);
 17488:                                 u64 part_copied = 0;
 17489:                                 while (part_copied < part_size)
 17490:                                 {
 17491:                                     u32 copy_offset = part_offset + (u32)part_copied;
 17492:                                     u64 remaining = part_size - part_copied;
 17493:                                     u32 chunk = codegen_canonical_copy_chunk(
 17494:                                         remaining, indirect ? copy_offset : value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value] + copy_offset,
 17495:                                         result_offset + copy_offset);
 17496:                                     if (indirect)
 17497:                                     {
 17498:                                         a64_emit_load_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 10, copy_offset, chunk);
 17499:                                     }
 17500:                                     else
 17501:                                     {
 17502:                                         a64_emit_load_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value] + copy_offset, chunk);
 17503:                                     }
 17504:                                     a64_emit_store_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, result_offset + copy_offset, chunk);
 17505:                                     part_copied += chunk;
 17506:                                 }
 17507:                             }
 17508:                         }
 17509:                         else if (indirect)
 17510:                         {
 17511:                             if (!loaded_type || !loaded_type->layout.resolved ||
 17512:                                 (loaded_type->layout.size != 1 && loaded_type->layout.size != 2 && loaded_type->layout.size != 4 &&
 17513:                                  loaded_type->layout.size != 8))
 17514:                             {
 17515:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 17516:                                 return result;
 17517:                             }
 17518:                             a64_emit_load_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value], 8);
 17519:                             a64_emit_load_pointer(&buffer, 9, 9, (u32)loaded_type->layout.size);
 17520:                         }
 17521:                         else
 17522:                         {
 17523:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
 17524:                         }
 17525:                         if (!aggregate)
 17526:                         {
 17527:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17528:                         }
 17529:                     }
 17530:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX)
 17531:                     {
 17532:                         IrValueId base = instruction->operands[0];
 17533:                         IrInstruction* base_definition = function->instructions + function->values[base.value].definition.value;
 17534:                         IrType* base_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[base.value].canonical_type);
 17535:                         u32 base_offset = value_offsets[base.value];
 17536:                         if (base_definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL || (function->values[base.value].category == IR_VALUE_VALUE && base_type &&
 17537:                                                                            (base_type->kind == IR_TYPE_ARRAY || base_type->kind == IR_TYPE_VECTOR)))
 17538:                         {
 17539:                             if (base_definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && function->values[base.value].alignment > 16)
 17540:                             {
 17541:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, base);
 17542:                             }
 17543:                             else
 17544:                             {
 17545:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, base_offset);
 17546:                             }
 17547:                         }
 17548:                         else
 17549:                         {
 17550:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, base);
 17551:                         }
 17552:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[1]);
 17553:                         IrType* index_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type);
 17554:                         IrType* element = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 17555:                         if (!index_type || index_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER || !element || !element->layout.resolved)
 17556:                         {
 17557:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 17558:                             return result;
 17559:                         }
 17560:                         if (index_type->is_signed && index_type->bit_width < 64)
 17561:                         {
 17562:                             u32 sign_extend = index_type->bit_width == 8    ? 0x93401d4a
 17563:                                               : index_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x93403d4a
 17564:                                               : index_type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x93407d4a
 17565:                                                                             : 0;
 17566:                             if (!sign_extend)
 17567:                             {
 17568:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 17569:                                 return result;
 17570:                             }
 17571:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, sign_extend);
 17572:                         }
 17573:                         // The stride is an arbitrary 64-bit constant, not a movz
 17574:                         // immediate: an element of 64 KiB or more needs the movk
 17575:                         // continuation, and used to be rejected outright.
 17576:                         a64_emit_constant_compact(&buffer, 11, element->layout.size);
 17577:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9b0b7d4a);
 17578:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8b0a0129);
 17579:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17580:                     }
 17581:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ADDRESS_OF)
 17582:                     {
 17583:                         IrValueId object = instruction->operands[0];
 17584:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + function->values[object.value].definition.value;
 17585:                         u32 object_offset = value_offsets[object.value];
 17586:                         if (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL)
 17587:                         {
 17588:                             if (function->values[object.value].alignment > 16)
 17589:                             {
 17590:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, object);
 17591:                             }
 17592:                             else
 17593:                             {
 17594:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, object_offset);
 17595:                             }
 17596:                         }
 17597:                         else
 17598:                         {
 17599:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, object);
 17600:                         }
 17601:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17602:                     }
 17603:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE)
 17604:                     {
 17605:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
 17606:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17607:                     }
 17608:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD)
 17609:                     {
 17610:                         IrValueId base = instruction->operands[0];
 17611:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + function->values[base.value].definition.value;
 17612:                         u32 base_offset = value_offsets[base.value];
 17613:                         if (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL)
 17614:                         {
 17615:                             if (function->values[base.value].alignment > 16)
 17616:                             {
 17617:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, base);
 17618:                             }
 17619:                             else
 17620:                             {
 17621:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, base_offset);
 17622:                             }
 17623:                         }
 17624:                         else
 17625:                         {
 17626:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, base);
 17627:                         }
 17628:                         IrType* aggregate = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[base.value].canonical_type);
 17629:                         u64 field_index = instruction->immediates[0];
 17630:                         if (!aggregate || field_index >= aggregate->field_count)
 17631:                         {
 17632:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 17633:                             return result;
 17634:                         }
 17635:                         u64 field_offset = aggregate->fields[field_index].offset;
 17636:                         if (field_offset <= A64_IMM12_MAX)
 17637:                         {
 17638:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91000129 | ((u32)field_offset << 10));
 17639:                         }
 17640:                         else
 17641:                         {
 17642:                             a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 10, field_offset);
 17643:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8b0a0129);
 17644:                         }
 17645:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17646:                     }
 17647:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CAST)
 17648:                     {
 17649:                         IrType* source_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
 17650:                         IrType* target_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 17651:                         IrConversionOperation conversion = instruction->conversion_operation;
 17652:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
 17653:                         if (!target_type || !source_type)
 17654:                         {
 17655:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 17656:                             return result;
 17657:                         }
 17658:                         bool source_integer128 = source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && source_type->bit_width == 128;
 17659:                         bool target_integer128 = target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && target_type->bit_width == 128;
 17660:                         if (target_integer128 && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
 17661:                             (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER))
 17662:                         {
 17663:                             bool converted = codegen_canonical_a64_float_to_i128(&buffer, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value], result_offset,
 17664:                                                                                   source_type->bit_width,
 17665:                                                                                   conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER);
 17666:                             if (!converted)
 17667:                             {
 17668:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 17669:                                 return result;
 17670:                             }
 17671:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 17672:                             continue;
 17673:                         }
 17674:                         if (target_integer128)
 17675:                         {
 17676:                             if (source_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
 17677:                             {
 17678:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 17679:                                 return result;
 17680:                             }
 17681:                             // x9 already holds the source's low eightbyte. A
 17682:                             // narrower source is widened in place first, then
 17683:                             // the second eightbyte is its sign or zero fill;
 17684:                             // a 128-bit source carries its own high half
 17685:                             // through unchanged.
 17686:                             if (source_integer128)
 17687:                             {
 17688:                                 c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
 17689:                             }
 17690:                             else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND)
 17691:                             {
 17692:                                 u32 narrow_sign_extend = source_type->bit_width == 8    ? 0x93401d29
 17693:                                                          : source_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x93403d29
 17694:                                                          : source_type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x93407d29
 17695:                                                                                         : 0;
 17696:                                 if (narrow_sign_extend)
 17697:                                 {
 17698:                                     codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, narrow_sign_extend);
 17699:                                 }
 17700:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x937ffd2a); // asr x10, x9, #63
 17701:                             }
 17702:                             else
 17703:                             {
 17704:                                 u32 narrow_zero_extend = source_type->bit_width == 8    ? 0x53001d29
 17705:                                                          : source_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x53003d29
 17706:                                                          : source_type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x2a0903e9
 17707:                                                                                         : 0;
 17708:                                 if (narrow_zero_extend)
 17709:                                 {
 17710:                                     codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, narrow_zero_extend);
 17711:                                 }
 17712:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa1f03ea); // mov x10, xzr
 17713:                             }
 17714:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17715:                             c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 10, result_offset);
 17716:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 17717:                             continue;
 17718:                         }
 17719:                         if (source_integer128 && target_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
 17720:                             !(target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
 17721:                               (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT)))
 17722:                         {
 17723:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 17724:                             return result;
 17725:                         }
 17726:                         if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
 17727:                         {
 17728:                             u32 sign_extend = source_type->bit_width == 8    ? 0x93401d29
 17729:                                               : source_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x93403d29
 17730:                                               : source_type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x93407d29
 17731:                                                                              : 0;
 17732:                             if (sign_extend)
 17733:                             {
 17734:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, sign_extend);
 17735:                             }
 17736:                         }
 17737:                         else if ((conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_ZERO_EXTEND || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_TRUNCATE ||
 17738:                                   conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_REINTERPRET) &&
 17739:                                  source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
 17740:                         {
 17741:                             u32 effective_bit_width = conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_ZERO_EXTEND ? source_type->bit_width
 17742:                                                       : conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_REINTERPRET && source_type->bit_width < target_type->bit_width
 17743:                                                           ? source_type->bit_width
 17744:                                                           : target_type->bit_width;
 17745:                             u32 zero_extend = effective_bit_width == 8    ? 0x53001d29
 17746:                                               : effective_bit_width == 16 ? 0x53003d29
 17747:                                               : effective_bit_width == 32 ? 0x2a0903e9
 17748:                                                                           : 0;
 17749:                             if (zero_extend)
 17750:                             {
 17751:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, zero_extend);
 17752:                             }
 17753:                         }
 17754:                         if (source_integer128 && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
 17755:                             (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT))
 17756:                         {
 17757:                             bool converted = codegen_canonical_a64_i128_to_float(&buffer, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value], result_offset,
 17758:                                                                                  target_type->bit_width,
 17759:                                                                                  conversion == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT);
 17760:                             if (!converted)
 17761:                             {
 17762:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 17763:                                 return result;
 17764:                             }
 17765:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 17766:                             continue;
 17767:                         }
 17768:                         if (target_type && source_type && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
 17769:                             (instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT ||
 17770:                              instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT))
 17771:                         {
 17772:                             if (instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT && source_type->bit_width < 64)
 17773:                             {
 17774:                                 u32 sign_extend = source_type->bit_width == 8 ? 0x93401d29 : source_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x93403d29 : 0x93407d29;
 17775:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, sign_extend);
 17776:                             }
 17777:                             u32 encoded = instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT ? 0x9e220000 : 0x9e230000;
 17778:                             if (target_type->bit_width == 64)
 17779:                             {
 17780:                                 encoded |= 0x00400000;
 17781:                             }
 17782:                             else if (target_type->bit_width != 32)
 17783:                             {
 17784:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 17785:                                 return result;
 17786:                             }
 17787:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, encoded | (9 << 5));
 17788:                             codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, result_offset, (u32)target_type->layout.size, true);
 17789:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 17790:                             continue;
 17791:                         }
 17792:                         if (target_type && source_type && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
 17793:                             (instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_EXTEND ||
 17794:                              instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TRUNCATE))
 17795:                         {
 17796:                             codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value],
 17797:                                                                                (u32)source_type->layout.size, false);
 17798:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_EXTEND ? 0x1e22c000 : 0x1e624000);
 17799:                             codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, result_offset, (u32)target_type->layout.size, true);
 17800:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 17801:                             continue;
 17802:                         }
 17803:                         if (target_type && source_type && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
 17804:                             (instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER ||
 17805:                              instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER))
 17806:                         {
 17807:                             codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value],
 17808:                                                                                (u32)source_type->layout.size, false);
 17809:                             u32 encoded = instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER ? 0x9e380000 : 0x9e390000;
 17810:                             if (source_type->bit_width == 64)
 17811:                             {
 17812:                                 encoded |= 0x00400000;
 17813:                             }
 17814:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, encoded | 9);
 17815:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17816:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 17817:                             continue;
 17818:                         }
 17819:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17820:                     }
 17821:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_STORE || instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE)
 17822:                     {
 17823:                         IrValue* place = function->values + instruction->operands[0].value;
 17824:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
 17825:                         u32 aggregate_parts = 0;
 17826:                         bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type,
 17827:                                                                                    &aggregate_parts);
 17828:                         IrType* stored_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type);
 17829:                         if (!aggregate && stored_type && stored_type->layout.resolved && stored_type->layout.size > 8 &&
 17830:                             stored_type->layout.size <= (u64)UINT32_MAX * 8)
 17831:                         {
 17832:                             aggregate = true;
 17833:                             aggregate_parts = (u32)((stored_type->layout.size + 7) / 8);
 17834:                         }
 17835:                         bool indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
 17836:                                         definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE ||
 17837:                                         (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && place->alignment > 16);
 17838:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE && aggregate)
 17506:                                 }
 17507:                             }
 17508:                         }
 17509:                         else if (indirect)
 17510:                         {
 17511:                             if (!loaded_type || !loaded_type->layout.resolved ||
 17512:                                 (loaded_type->layout.size != 1 && loaded_type->layout.size != 2 && loaded_type->layout.size != 4 &&
 17513:                                  loaded_type->layout.size != 8))
 17514:                             {
 17515:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 17516:                                 return result;
 17517:                             }
 17518:                             a64_emit_load_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value], 8);
 17519:                             a64_emit_load_pointer(&buffer, 9, 9, (u32)loaded_type->layout.size);
 17520:                         }
 17521:                         else
 17522:                         {
 17523:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
 17524:                         }
 17525:                         if (!aggregate)
 17526:                         {
 17527:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17528:                         }
 17529:                     }
 17530:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX)
 17531:                     {
 17532:                         IrValueId base = instruction->operands[0];
 17533:                         IrInstruction* base_definition = function->instructions + function->values[base.value].definition.value;
 17534:                         IrType* base_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[base.value].canonical_type);
 17535:                         u32 base_offset = value_offsets[base.value];
 17536:                         if (base_definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL || (function->values[base.value].category == IR_VALUE_VALUE && base_type &&
 17537:                                                                            (base_type->kind == IR_TYPE_ARRAY || base_type->kind == IR_TYPE_VECTOR)))
 17538:                         {
 17539:                             if (base_definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && function->values[base.value].alignment > 16)
 17540:                             {
 17541:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, base);
 17542:                             }
 17543:                             else
 17544:                             {
 17545:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, base_offset);
 17546:                             }
 17547:                         }
 17548:                         else
 17549:                         {
 17550:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, base);
 17551:                         }
 17552:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[1]);
 17553:                         IrType* index_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type);
 17554:                         IrType* element = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 17555:                         if (!index_type || index_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER || !element || !element->layout.resolved)
 17556:                         {
 17557:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 17558:                             return result;
 17559:                         }
 17560:                         if (index_type->is_signed && index_type->bit_width < 64)
 17561:                         {
 17562:                             u32 sign_extend = index_type->bit_width == 8    ? 0x93401d4a
 17563:                                               : index_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x93403d4a
 17564:                                               : index_type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x93407d4a
 17565:                                                                             : 0;
 17566:                             if (!sign_extend)
 17567:                             {
 17568:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 17569:                                 return result;
 17570:                             }
 17571:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, sign_extend);
 17572:                         }
 17573:                         // The stride is an arbitrary 64-bit constant, not a movz
 17574:                         // immediate: an element of 64 KiB or more needs the movk
 17575:                         // continuation, and used to be rejected outright.
 17576:                         a64_emit_constant_compact(&buffer, 11, element->layout.size);
 17577:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9b0b7d4a);
 17578:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8b0a0129);
 17579:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17580:                     }
 17581:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ADDRESS_OF)
 17582:                     {
 17583:                         IrValueId object = instruction->operands[0];
 17584:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + function->values[object.value].definition.value;
 17585:                         u32 object_offset = value_offsets[object.value];
 17586:                         if (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL)
 17587:                         {
 17588:                             if (function->values[object.value].alignment > 16)
 17589:                             {
 17590:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, object);
 17591:                             }
 17592:                             else
 17593:                             {
 17594:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, object_offset);
 17595:                             }
 17596:                         }
 17597:                         else
 17598:                         {
 17599:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, object);
 17600:                         }
 17601:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17602:                     }
 17603:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE)
 17604:                     {
 17605:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
 17606:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17607:                     }
 17608:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD)
 17609:                     {
 17610:                         IrValueId base = instruction->operands[0];
 17611:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + function->values[base.value].definition.value;
 17612:                         u32 base_offset = value_offsets[base.value];
 17613:                         if (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL)
 17614:                         {
 17615:                             if (function->values[base.value].alignment > 16)
 17616:                             {
 17617:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, base);
 17618:                             }
 17619:                             else
 17620:                             {
 17621:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, base_offset);
 17622:                             }
 17623:                         }
 17624:                         else
 17625:                         {
 17626:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, base);
 17627:                         }
 17628:                         IrType* aggregate = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[base.value].canonical_type);
 17629:                         u64 field_index = instruction->immediates[0];
 17630:                         if (!aggregate || field_index >= aggregate->field_count)
 17631:                         {
 17632:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 17633:                             return result;
 17634:                         }
 17635:                         u64 field_offset = aggregate->fields[field_index].offset;
 17636:                         if (field_offset <= A64_IMM12_MAX)
 17637:                         {
 17638:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91000129 | ((u32)field_offset << 10));
 17639:                         }
 17640:                         else
 17641:                         {
 17642:                             a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 10, field_offset);
 17643:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8b0a0129);
 17644:                         }
 17645:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17646:                     }
 17647:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CAST)
 17648:                     {
 17649:                         IrType* source_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
 17650:                         IrType* target_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 17651:                         IrConversionOperation conversion = instruction->conversion_operation;
 17652:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
 17653:                         if (!target_type || !source_type)
 17654:                         {
 17655:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 17656:                             return result;
 17657:                         }
 17658:                         bool source_integer128 = source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && source_type->bit_width == 128;
 17659:                         bool target_integer128 = target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && target_type->bit_width == 128;
 17660:                         if (target_integer128 && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
 17661:                             (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER))
 17662:                         {
 17663:                             bool converted = codegen_canonical_a64_float_to_i128(&buffer, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value], result_offset,
 17664:                                                                                   source_type->bit_width,
 17665:                                                                                   conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER);
 17666:                             if (!converted)
 17667:                             {
 17668:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 17669:                                 return result;
 17670:                             }
 17671:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 17672:                             continue;
 17673:                         }
 17674:                         if (target_integer128)
 17675:                         {
 17676:                             if (source_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
 17677:                             {
 17678:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 17679:                                 return result;
 17680:                             }
 17681:                             // x9 already holds the source's low eightbyte. A
 17682:                             // narrower source is widened in place first, then
 17683:                             // the second eightbyte is its sign or zero fill;
 17684:                             // a 128-bit source carries its own high half
 17685:                             // through unchanged.
 17686:                             if (source_integer128)
 17687:                             {
 17688:                                 c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
 17689:                             }
 17690:                             else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND)
 17691:                             {
 17692:                                 u32 narrow_sign_extend = source_type->bit_width == 8    ? 0x93401d29
 17693:                                                          : source_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x93403d29
 17694:                                                          : source_type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x93407d29
 17695:                                                                                         : 0;
 17696:                                 if (narrow_sign_extend)
 17697:                                 {
 17698:                                     codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, narrow_sign_extend);
 17699:                                 }
 17700:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x937ffd2a); // asr x10, x9, #63
 17701:                             }
 17702:                             else
 17703:                             {
 17704:                                 u32 narrow_zero_extend = source_type->bit_width == 8    ? 0x53001d29
 17705:                                                          : source_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x53003d29
 17706:                                                          : source_type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x2a0903e9
 17707:                                                                                         : 0;
 17708:                                 if (narrow_zero_extend)
 17709:                                 {
 17710:                                     codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, narrow_zero_extend);
 17711:                                 }
 17712:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa1f03ea); // mov x10, xzr
 17713:                             }
 17714:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17715:                             c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 10, result_offset);
 17716:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 17717:                             continue;
 17718:                         }
 17719:                         if (source_integer128 && target_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
 17720:                             !(target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
 17721:                               (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT)))
 17722:                         {
 17723:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 17724:                             return result;
 17725:                         }
 17726:                         if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
 17727:                         {
 17728:                             u32 sign_extend = source_type->bit_width == 8    ? 0x93401d29
 17729:                                               : source_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x93403d29
 17730:                                               : source_type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x93407d29
 17731:                                                                              : 0;
 17732:                             if (sign_extend)
 17733:                             {
 17734:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, sign_extend);
 17735:                             }
 17736:                         }
 17737:                         else if ((conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_ZERO_EXTEND || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_TRUNCATE ||
 17738:                                   conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_REINTERPRET) &&
 17739:                                  source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
 17740:                         {
 17741:                             u32 effective_bit_width = conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_ZERO_EXTEND ? source_type->bit_width
 17742:                                                       : conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_REINTERPRET && source_type->bit_width < target_type->bit_width
 17743:                                                           ? source_type->bit_width
 17744:                                                           : target_type->bit_width;
 17745:                             u32 zero_extend = effective_bit_width == 8    ? 0x53001d29
 17746:                                               : effective_bit_width == 16 ? 0x53003d29
 17747:                                               : effective_bit_width == 32 ? 0x2a0903e9
 17748:                                                                           : 0;
 17749:                             if (zero_extend)
 17750:                             {
 17751:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, zero_extend);
 17752:                             }
 17753:                         }
 17754:                         if (source_integer128 && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
 17755:                             (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT))
 17756:                         {
 17757:                             bool converted = codegen_canonical_a64_i128_to_float(&buffer, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value], result_offset,
 17758:                                                                                  target_type->bit_width,
 17759:                                                                                  conversion == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT);
 17760:                             if (!converted)
 17761:                             {
 17762:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 17763:                                 return result;
 17764:                             }
 17765:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 17766:                             continue;
 17767:                         }
 17768:                         if (target_type && source_type && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
 17769:                             (instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT ||
 17770:                              instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT))
 17771:                         {
 17772:                             if (instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT && source_type->bit_width < 64)
 17773:                             {
 17774:                                 u32 sign_extend = source_type->bit_width == 8 ? 0x93401d29 : source_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x93403d29 : 0x93407d29;
 17775:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, sign_extend);
 17776:                             }
 17777:                             u32 encoded = instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT ? 0x9e220000 : 0x9e230000;
 17778:                             if (target_type->bit_width == 64)
 17779:                             {
 17780:                                 encoded |= 0x00400000;
 17781:                             }
 17782:                             else if (target_type->bit_width != 32)
 17783:                             {
 17784:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 17785:                                 return result;
 17786:                             }
 17787:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, encoded | (9 << 5));
 17788:                             codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, result_offset, (u32)target_type->layout.size, true);
 17789:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 17790:                             continue;
 17791:                         }
 17792:                         if (target_type && source_type && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
 17793:                             (instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_EXTEND ||
 17794:                              instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TRUNCATE))
 17795:                         {
 17796:                             codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value],
 17797:                                                                                (u32)source_type->layout.size, false);
 17798:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_EXTEND ? 0x1e22c000 : 0x1e624000);
 17799:                             codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, result_offset, (u32)target_type->layout.size, true);
 17800:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 17801:                             continue;
 17802:                         }
 17803:                         if (target_type && source_type && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
 17804:                             (instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER ||
 17805:                              instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER))
 17806:                         {
 17807:                             codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value],
 17808:                                                                                (u32)source_type->layout.size, false);
 17809:                             u32 encoded = instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER ? 0x9e380000 : 0x9e390000;
 17810:                             if (source_type->bit_width == 64)
 17811:                             {
 17812:                                 encoded |= 0x00400000;
 17813:                             }
 17814:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, encoded | 9);
 17815:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 17816:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 17817:                             continue;
 17818:                         }
