  1044: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool codegen_inline_assembly_constraint_register(u64 constraint, X64Register* register_out)
  1045: {
  1046:     switch (constraint & 0xff)
  1047:     {
  1048:     case IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_A:
  1049:         *register_out = X64_REGISTER_RAX;
  1050:         return true;
  1051:     case IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_B:
  1052:         *register_out = X64_REGISTER_RBX;
  1053:         return true;
  1054:     case IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_C:
  1055:         *register_out = X64_REGISTER_RCX;
  1056:         return true;
  1057:     case IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_D:
  1058:         *register_out = X64_REGISTER_RDX;
  1059:         return true;
  1060:     case IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_SI:
  1061:         *register_out = X64_REGISTER_RSI;
  1062:         return true;
  1063:     case IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_DI:
  1064:         *register_out = X64_REGISTER_RDI;
  1065:         return true;
  1066:     case IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_R8:
  1067:         *register_out = X64_REGISTER_R8;
  1068:         return true;
  1069:     case IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_R9:
  1070:         *register_out = X64_REGISTER_R9;
  1071:         return true;
  1072:     case IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_R10:
  1073:         *register_out = X64_REGISTER_R10;
  1074:         return true;
  1075:     case IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_R11:
  1076:         *register_out = X64_REGISTER_R11;
  1077:         return true;
  1078:     case IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_R:
  1079:     case IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_COUNT:
  1080:         break;
  1081:     }
  1082:     return false;
  1083: }
  1084: 
  1085: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL u32 codegen_inline_assembly_type_class(IrType* type)
  1086: {
  1087:     if (type && type->layout.resolved && type->layout.size && type->layout.size <= 8)
  1088:     {
  1089:         switch (type->kind)
  1090:         {
  1091:         case IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN:
  1092:         case IR_TYPE_INTEGER:
  1093:         case IR_TYPE_ENUM:
  1094:             return IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_OPERAND_CLASS_INTEGER;
  1095:         case IR_TYPE_POINTER:
  1096:             return IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_OPERAND_CLASS_POINTER;
  1097:         case IR_TYPE_VOID:
  1098:         case IR_TYPE_FLOAT:
  1099:         case IR_TYPE_VA_LIST:
  1100:         case IR_TYPE_SLICE:
  1101:         case IR_TYPE_ARRAY:
  1102:         case IR_TYPE_VECTOR:
  1103:         case IR_TYPE_FUNCTION:
  1104:         case IR_TYPE_RANGE:
  1105:         case IR_TYPE_STRUCT:
  1106:         case IR_TYPE_UNION:
  1107:         case IR_TYPE_COUNT:
  1108:             break;
  1109:         }
  1110:     }
  1111: 
  1112:     return IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_OPERAND_CLASS_INVALID;
  1113: }
  1114: 
  1115: // What GNU's 'x' may carry, and the mirror of c_ir_inline_assembly_vector_operand
  1116: // in the frontend: a float of the width the scalar SSE instructions operate on.
  1117: // The x87 `long double` is a different register file and is not one of these.
  1118: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool codegen_inline_assembly_vector_operand(IrType* type)
  1119: {
  1120:     return type && type->layout.resolved && type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && (type->layout.size == 4 || type->layout.size == 8);
  1121: }
  1122: 
  1123: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool codegen_inline_assembly_types_compatible(IrType* output, IrType* input)
  1124: {
  1125:     u32 output_class = codegen_inline_assembly_type_class(output);
  1126:     u32 input_class = codegen_inline_assembly_type_class(input);
  1127:     return output_class != IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_OPERAND_CLASS_INVALID && output_class == input_class && output->layout.size == input->layout.size;
  1128: }
  1129: 
  1130: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool codegen_inline_assembly_constraint_shape_valid(u64 constraint, u32 operand_index, u32 operand_count, u32* match_index_out)
  1131: {
  1132:     if ((constraint & ~IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_KNOWN_MASK) ||
  1133:         (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK) >= IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_COUNT)
  1134:     {
  1135:         return false;
  1136:     }
  1137:     bool output = (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_OUTPUT) != 0;
  1138:     bool read_write = (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_READ_WRITE) != 0;
  1139:     bool matching = (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH) != 0;
  1140:     if ((read_write && !output) || (matching && (output || read_write)))
  1141:     {
  1142:         return false;
  1143:     }
  1144:     if (!matching)
  1145:     {
  1146:         return (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH_INDEX_MASK) == 0;
  1147:     }
  1148:     u32 match_index = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH_INDEX(constraint);
  1149:     if (match_index >= operand_index || match_index >= operand_count)
  1150:     {
  1151:         return false;
  1152:     }
  1153:     if (match_index_out)
  1154:     {
  1155:         *match_index_out = match_index;
  1156:     }
  1157:     return true;
  1158: }
  1159: 
  1160: // The atomic and inline-assembly rows this asks about are rare — six of the
  1161: // 3,814 functions in a self-compile hold one — so the opcode summary the
  1162: // builder already accumulated answers for every other function without
  1163: // reading a single row.
  1164: #define CODEGEN_X64_SHAPE_OPCODES                                                                                                      \
  1165:     (IR_OPCODE_BIT(IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_LOAD) | IR_OPCODE_BIT(IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE) | IR_OPCODE_BIT(IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_READ_MODIFY_WRITE) | \
  1166:      IR_OPCODE_BIT(IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_COMPARE_EXCHANGE) | IR_OPCODE_BIT(IR_OPCODE_INLINE_ASSEMBLY))
  1167: 
  1168: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool codegen_canonical_x64_function_shape(IrFunction* function, bool* saves_rbx)
  1169: {
  1170:     *saves_rbx = false;
  1171:     if (function && ir_function_may_contain_opcodes(function, CODEGEN_X64_SHAPE_OPCODES))
  1172:     {
  1173:         for (u32 instruction_index = 0; instruction_index < function->instruction_count; instruction_index += 1)
  1174:         {
  1175:             IrInstruction* instruction = function->instructions + instruction_index;
  1176:             if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_LOAD || instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE ||
  1177:                 instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_READ_MODIFY_WRITE || instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_COMPARE_EXCHANGE)
  1178:             {
  1179:                 *saves_rbx = true;
  1180:                 return true;
  1181:             }
  1182:             if (instruction->opcode != IR_OPCODE_INLINE_ASSEMBLY)
  1183:             {
  1184:                 continue;
  1185:             }
  1186:             IrInstructionExtra extra = ir_instruction_extra(function, ir_instruction_self_id(function, instruction));
  1187:             if ((instruction->operand_count && !instruction->immediates) || (extra.clobber_count && !extra.clobbers))
  1188:             {
  1189:                 return false;
  1190:             }
  1191:             for (u32 operand_index = 0; operand_index < instruction->operand_count; operand_index += 1)
  1192:             {
  1193:                 if ((instruction->immediates[operand_index] & 0xff) == IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_B)
  1194:                 {
  1195:                     *saves_rbx = true;
  1196:                     return true;
  1197:                 }
  1198:             }
  1199:             for (u32 clobber_index = 0; clobber_index < extra.clobber_count; clobber_index += 1)
  1200:             {
  1201:                 if (codegen_inline_assembly_clobber_is_rbx(extra.clobbers[clobber_index]))
  1202:                 {
  1203:                     *saves_rbx = true;
  1204:                     return true;
  1205:                 }
  1206:             }
  1207:         }
  1208:     }
  1209: 
  1210:     return true;
  1211: }
  1212: 
  1213: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool codegen_canonical_x64_asm_memory_width(u32 width)
  1214: {
  1215:     return width == 1 || width == 2 || width == 4 || width == 8;
  1216: }
  1217: 
  1218: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool codegen_canonical_a64_asm_memory_width(u32 width)
  1219: {
  1220:     return width == 1 || width == 2 || width == 4 || width == 8;
  1221: }
  1222: 
  1223: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL u32 codegen_canonical_a64_nop_count(String8 literal)
  1224: {
  1225:     u32 count = 0;
  1226:     u64 offset = 0;
  1227:     while (offset < literal.length)
  1228:     {
  1229:         while (offset < literal.length && (literal.pointer[offset] == ' ' || literal.pointer[offset] == '\t' || literal.pointer[offset] == '\r' ||
  1230:                                             literal.pointer[offset] == '\n'))
  1231:         {
  1232:             offset += 1;
  1233:         }
  1234:         while (offset + 1 < literal.length && literal.pointer[offset] == '\\' &&
  1235:                (literal.pointer[offset + 1] == 'n' || literal.pointer[offset + 1] == 'r' || literal.pointer[offset + 1] == 't'))
  1236:         {
  1237:             offset += 2;
  1238:             while (offset < literal.length && (literal.pointer[offset] == ' ' || literal.pointer[offset] == '\t' || literal.pointer[offset] == '\r' ||
  1239:                                                 literal.pointer[offset] == '\n'))
  1240:             {
  1241:                 offset += 1;
  1242:             }
  1243:         }
  1244:         if (offset == literal.length || literal.pointer[offset] == 0)
  1245:         {
  1246:             break;
  1247:         }
  1248:         if (offset + 3 > literal.length || literal.pointer[offset] != 'n' || literal.pointer[offset + 1] != 'o' || literal.pointer[offset + 2] != 'p')
  1249:         {
  1250:             return 0;
  1251:         }
  1252:         offset += 3;
  1253:         if (count == UINT32_MAX)
  1254:         {
  1255:             return 0;
  1256:         }
  1257:         count += 1;
  1258:     }
  1259:     return count;
  1260: }
  1261: 
  1262: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64Register register_index, u16 width)
  1263: {
  1264:     return (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand){
  1265:         .kind = BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_OPERAND_REGISTER,
  1266:         .width = width,
  1267:         .reg = {
  1268:             .index = (u16)register_index,
  1269:             .width = width,
  1270:             .physical_class = BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_GPR,
  1271:         },
  1272:     };
  1273: }
  1274: 
  1275: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(u32 register_index, u16 width)
  1276: {
  1277:     u8 physical_class = width <= 128 ? BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_XMM
  1278:                                      : width == 256 ? BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_YMM
  1279:                                                     : BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_ZMM;
  1280:     return (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand){
  1281:         .kind = BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_OPERAND_REGISTER,
  1282:         .width = width,
  1283:         .reg = {
  1284:             .index = (u16)register_index,
  1285:             .width = width,
  1286:             .physical_class = physical_class,
  1287:         },
  1288:     };
  1289: }
  1290: 
  1291: // One x87 stack slot, ST(index).  The metadata tables carry ST0..ST7 as
  1292: // SPECIAL registers of architectural width 80, which is also how the
  1293: // assembler's own crosswalk spells ASSEMBLY_REGISTER_X87.
  1294: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_x87(u32 register_index)
  1295: {
  1296:     return (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand){
  1297:         .kind = BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_OPERAND_REGISTER,
  1298:         .width = 80,
  1299:         .reg = {
  1300:             .index = (u16)register_index,
  1301:             .width = 80,
  1302:             .physical_class = BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_SPECIAL,
  1303:         },
  1304:     };
  1305: }
  1306: 
  1307: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(s64 value, u16 width)
  1308: {
  1309:     return (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand){
  1310:         .kind = BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_OPERAND_IMMEDIATE,
  1311:         .width = width,
  1312:         .value = value,
  1313:         .has_value = true,
  1314:     };
  1315: }
  1316: 
  1317: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(u64 value, u16 width)
  1318: {
  1319:     return (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand){
  1320:         .kind = BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_OPERAND_IMMEDIATE,
  1321:         .width = width,
  1322:         .unsigned_value = value,
  1323:         .has_unsigned_value = true,
  1324:     };
  1325: }
  1326: 
  1327: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(s64 value, u16 width)
  1328: {
  1329:     return (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand){
  1330:         .kind = BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_OPERAND_RELATIVE,
  1331:         .width = width,
  1332:         .value = value,
  1333:         .has_value = true,
  1334:     };
  1335: }
  1336: 
  1337: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64Register base, u16 width, s64 displacement)
  1338: {
  1339:     return (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand){
  1340:         .kind = BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_OPERAND_MEMORY,
  1341:         .width = width,
  1342:         .memory = {
  1343:             .base = {
  1344:                 .index = (u16)base,
  1345:                 .width = 64,
  1346:                 .physical_class = BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_GPR,
  1347:             },
  1348:             .displacement = displacement,
  1349:             .address_size = 64,
  1350:             .scale = 1,
  1351:             .has_base = true,
  1352:             // Keep the frame/reference displacement explicit.  In
  1353:             // particular, [rbp] without a displacement is not the same
  1354:             // ModRM shape as [rbp+0], because rm=5 is RIP-relative in
  1355:             // address modes that omit a displacement.
  1356:             .has_displacement = true,
  1357:         },
 12553:                             {
 12554:                             case IR_ATOMIC_ADD:
 12555:                                 low_mnemonic = S8("ADD");
 12556:                                 high_mnemonic = S8("ADC");
 12557:                                 break;
 12558:                             case IR_ATOMIC_SUBTRACT:
 12559:                                 low_mnemonic = S8("SUB");
 12560:                                 high_mnemonic = S8("SBB");
 12561:                                 break;
 12562:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_AND:
 12563:                                 low_mnemonic = S8("AND");
 12564:                                 high_mnemonic = S8("AND");
 12565:                                 break;
 12566:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_OR:
 12567:                                 low_mnemonic = S8("OR");
 12568:                                 high_mnemonic = S8("OR");
 12569:                                 break;
 12570:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_XOR:
 12571:                                 low_mnemonic = S8("XOR");
 12572:                                 high_mnemonic = S8("XOR");
 12573:                                 break;
 12574:                             case IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE:
 12575:                                 low_mnemonic = S8("MOV");
 12576:                                 high_mnemonic = S8("MOV");
 12577:                                 break;
 12578:                             case IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT:
 12579:                                 break;
 12580:                             }
 12581:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand low_operands[2] = {
 12582:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RBX, 64),
 12583:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R8, 64),
 12584:                             };
 12585:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand high_operands[2] = {
 12586:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
 12587:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R9, 64),
 12588:                             };
 12589:                             String8 cx16_features_names[] = {S8("cx16")};
 12590:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand cx16_memory = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_R10, 128, 0);
 12591:                             if (!low_mnemonic.length || !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, low_mnemonic, low_operands,
 12592:                                                                                                   BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(low_operands)) ||
 12593:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, high_mnemonic, high_operands,
 12594:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(high_operands)) ||
 12595:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_attributes(
 12596:                                     &buffer, S8("CMPXCHG16B"), &cx16_memory, 1,
 12597:                                     (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = cx16_features_names, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(cx16_features_names)},
 12598:                                     (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalAttributes){.lock = true}))
 12599:                             {
 12600:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12601:                                 return result;
 12602:                             }
 12603:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand retry_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
 12604:                             u32 retry_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 12605:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JNZ"), &retry_operand, 1))
 12606:                             {
 12607:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12608:                                 return result;
 12609:                             }
 12610:                             s64 retry_displacement = (s64)retry - (s64)buffer.count;
 12611:                             if (retry_displacement < INT32_MIN || retry_displacement > INT32_MAX || !buffer.bytes)
 12612:                             {
 12613:                                 result.error = retry_displacement < INT32_MIN || retry_displacement > INT32_MAX ? CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY
 12614:                                                                                                                  : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12615:                                 return result;
 12616:                             }
 12617:                             s32 retry_delta = (s32)retry_displacement;
 12618:                             memcpy(buffer.bytes + retry_branch_offset + 2, &retry_delta, sizeof(retry_delta));
 12619:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12620:                             c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12621:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 12622:                             continue;
 12623:                         }
 12624:                         if (!value_type ||
 12625:                             (!pointer_arithmetic && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
 12626:                              (instruction->atomic_operation != IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE ||
 12627:                               (value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_POINTER))) ||
 12628:                             !value_type->layout.resolved ||
 12629:                             (value_type->layout.size != 1 && value_type->layout.size != 2 && value_type->layout.size != 4 && value_type->layout.size != 8) ||
 12630:                             instruction->atomic_operation >= IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT)
 12631:                         {
 12632:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12633:                             return result;
 12634:                         }
 12635:                         if (indirect)
 12636:                         {
 12637:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[0]);
 12638:                         }
 12639:                         else
 12640:                         {
 12641:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand address_operands[2] = {
 12642:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
 12643:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
 12644:                                     X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value])),
 12645:                             };
 12646:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("LEA"), address_operands,
 12647:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(address_operands));
 12648:                         }
 12649:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x8d, instruction->operands[1]);
 12650:                         if (buffer.error)
 12651:                         {
 12652:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12653:                             return result;
 12654:                         }
 12655:                         codegen_canonical_x64_asm_load(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_RAX, X64_REGISTER_RDX, 0, (u32)value_type->layout.size);
 12656:                         if (buffer.error)
 12657:                         {
 12658:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12659:                             return result;
 12660:                         }
 12661:                         u16 atomic_width = (u16)(value_type->layout.size * 8);
 12662:                         u32 retry_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 12663:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand move_old_operands[2] = {
 12664:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R8, atomic_width),
 12665:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, atomic_width),
 12666:                         };
 12667:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), move_old_operands,
 12668:                                                                   BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(move_old_operands)))
 12669:                         {
 12670:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12671:                             return result;
 12672:                         }
 12673:                         String8 operation_mnemonic = {0};
 12674:                         if (instruction->atomic_operation == IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE)
 12675:                         {
 12676:                             operation_mnemonic = S8("MOV");
 12677:                         }
 12678:                         else
 12679:                         {
 12680:                             switch (instruction->atomic_operation)
 12681:                             {
 12682:                             case IR_ATOMIC_ADD:
 12683:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("ADD");
 12684:                                 break;
 12685:                             case IR_ATOMIC_SUBTRACT:
 12686:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("SUB");
 12687:                                 break;
 12688:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_AND:
 12689:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("AND");
 12690:                                 break;
 12691:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_OR:
 12692:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("OR");
 12693:                                 break;
 12694:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_XOR:
 12695:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("XOR");
 12696:                                 break;
 12697:                             case IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE:
 12698:                             case IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT:
 12699:                                 break;
 12700:                             }
 12701:                         }
 12702:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand operation_operands[2] = {
 12703:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R8, atomic_width),
 12704:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, atomic_width),
 12705:                         };
 12706:                         if (!operation_mnemonic.length ||
 12707:                             !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, operation_mnemonic, operation_operands,
 12708:                                                                   BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(operation_operands)) ||
 12709:                             !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_atomic_register_memory(&buffer, S8("CMPXCHG"), X64_REGISTER_RDX,
 12710:                                                                                       X64_REGISTER_R8, atomic_width))
 12711:                         {
 12712:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12713:                             return result;
 12714:                         }
 12715:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand retry_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
 12716:                         u32 retry_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 12717:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JNZ"), &retry_operand, 1))
 12718:                         {
 12719:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12720:                             return result;
 12721:                         }
 12722:                         s64 retry_displacement = (s64)retry_offset - (s64)buffer.count;
 12723:                         if (retry_displacement < INT32_MIN || retry_displacement > INT32_MAX || !buffer.bytes)
 12724:                         {
 12725:                             result.error = retry_displacement < INT32_MIN || retry_displacement > INT32_MAX ? CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY
 12726:                                                                                                              : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12727:                             return result;
 12728:                         }
 12729:                         s32 retry_delta = (s32)retry_displacement;
 12730:                         memcpy(buffer.bytes + retry_branch_offset + 2, &retry_delta, sizeof(retry_delta));
 12731:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12732:                     }
 12733:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_COMPARE_EXCHANGE)
 12734:                     {
 12735:                         IrValue* place = function->values + instruction->operands[0].value;
 12736:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
 12737:                         IrType* value_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 12738:                         bool indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
 12739:                                         definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE ||
 12740:                                         (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && place->alignment > 16);
 12741:                         if (value_type && value_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->layout.resolved && value_type->layout.size == 16)
 12742:                         {
 12743:                             if (!target_cpu_feature_has(target, TARGET_CPU_FEATURE_X86_CX16))
 12744:                             {
 12745:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12746:                                 return result;
 12747:                             }
 12748:                             c_x64_atomic_address(&emitter, instruction->operands[0], indirect);
 12749:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[1]);
 12750:                             c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[1]);
 12751:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x9d, instruction->operands[2]);
 12752:                             c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x8d, instruction->operands[2]);
 12753:                             String8 cx16_features_names[] = {S8("cx16")};
 12754:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand cx16_memory = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_R10, 128, 0);
 12755:                             if (buffer.error ||
 12756:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_attributes(
 12757:                                     &buffer, S8("CMPXCHG16B"), &cx16_memory, 1,
 12758:                                     (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = cx16_features_names, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(cx16_features_names)},
 12759:                                     (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalAttributes){.lock = true}))
 12760:                             {
 12761:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12762:                                 return result;
 12763:                             }
 12764:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12765:                             c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12766:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 12767:                             continue;
 12768:                         }
 12769:                         if (!value_type || (value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_POINTER) || !value_type->layout.resolved ||
 12770:                             (value_type->layout.size != 1 && value_type->layout.size != 2 && value_type->layout.size != 4 && value_type->layout.size != 8))
 12771:                         {
 12772:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12773:                             return result;
 12774:                         }
 12775:                         if (indirect)
 12776:                         {
 12777:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[0]);
 12778:                         }
 12779:                         else
 12780:                         {
 12781:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand address_operands[2] = {
 12782:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
 12783:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
 12784:                                     X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value])),
 12785:                             };
 12786:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("LEA"), address_operands,
 12787:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(address_operands));
 12788:                         }
 12789:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[1]);
 12790:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x8d, instruction->operands[2]);
 12791:                         if (buffer.error)
 12792:                         {
 12793:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12794:                             return result;
 12795:                         }
 12796:                         u16 atomic_width = (u16)(value_type->layout.size * 8);
 12797:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_atomic_register_memory(&buffer, S8("CMPXCHG"), X64_REGISTER_RDX,
 12798:                                                                                      X64_REGISTER_RCX, atomic_width))
 12799:                         {
 12800:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12801:                             return result;
 12802:                         }
 12803:                         if (value_type->layout.size == 1 || value_type->layout.size == 2)
 12804:                         {
 12805:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand widen_operands[2] = {
 12806:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
 12807:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, atomic_width),
 12808:                             };
 12809:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOVZX"), widen_operands,
 12810:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(widen_operands)))
 12811:                             {
 12812:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12813:                                 return result;
 12814:                             }
 12815:                         }
 12816:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12817:                     }
 12818:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_FENCE)
 12819:                     {
 12820:                         if (!instruction->atomic_signal_fence && instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL)
 12821:                         {
 12822:                             String8 fence_features[] = {S8("sse2")};
 12823:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
 12824:                                     &buffer, S8("MFENCE"), 0, 0,
 12825:                                     (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = fence_features, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(fence_features)}))
 12826:                             {
 12827:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12828:                                 return result;
 12829:                             }
 12830:                         }
 12831:                     }
 12832:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CLEAR_INSTRUCTION_CACHE)
 12833:                     {
 12834:                     }
 12835:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_INTEGER || instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_FLOAT)
 12836:                     {
 12837:                         IrType* constant_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 12838:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_FLOAT && constant_type && constant_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && constant_type->bit_width == 80)
 12839:                         {
 12840:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80(constant_type) || result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V ||
 12841:                                 instruction->immediate_count != 2 ||
 12842:                                 (instruction->immediates[1] & ~UINT64_C(0xffff)) ||
 12843:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_store_f80_constant(&buffer, result_displacement, instruction->immediates[0],
 12844:                                                                            (u16)instruction->immediates[1]))
 12845:                             {
 12846:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12847:                                 return result;
 12848:                             }
 12849:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 12850:                             continue;
 12851:                         }
 12852:                         u64 immediate = instruction->immediates[0];
 12853:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_INTEGER && instruction->immediate_is_negative)
 12854:                         {
 12855:                             immediate = 0 - immediate;
 12856:                         }
 12857:                         u16 constant_width = codegen_canonical_register_is_64_bit(program, instruction->canonical_type) ? 64 : 32;
 12858:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand constant_value =
 12859:                             constant_width == 32 && immediate > UINT32_MAX
 12860:                                 ? codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate((s64)(s32)(u32)immediate, constant_width)
 12861:                                 : codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(immediate, constant_width);
 12862:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand constant_operands[2] = {
 12863:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, constant_width),
 12864:                             constant_value,
 12865:                         };
 12866:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), constant_operands,
 12867:                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(constant_operands)))
 12868:                         {
 12869:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12870:                             return result;
 12871:                         }
 12872:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12873:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_INTEGER && constant_type && constant_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
 12874:                             constant_type->bit_width == 128)
 12875:                         {
 12876:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand high_constant_operands[2] = {
 12877:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
 12878:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(
 12879:                                     instruction->immediate_count > 1 ? instruction->immediates[1]
 12880:                                                                       : instruction->immediate_is_negative ? UINT64_MAX : 0,
 12881:                                     64),
 12882:                             };
 12883:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), high_constant_operands,
 12884:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(high_constant_operands)))
 12885:                             {
 12886:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12887:                                 return result;
 12888:                             }
 12889:                             c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12890:                         }
 12891:                     }
 12892:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_VA_START)
 12893:                     {
 12894:                         if (!canonical_variadic)
 12895:                         {
 12896:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 12897:                             return result;
 12898:                         }
 12899:                         u32 va_list_component_count = codegen_canonical_va_list_component_count(program, instruction->canonical_type);
 12900:                         if (!va_list_component_count)
 12901:                         {
 12902:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 12903:                             return result;
 12904:                         }
 12905:                         u32 gp_count = 0;
 12906:                         u32 fp_count = 0;
 12907:                         u32 stack_parts = 0;
 12908:                         for (u32 parameter_index = 0; parameter_index < canonical_function_type->parameter_count; parameter_index += 1)
 12909:                         {
 12910:                             IrTypeId parameter_type_id = canonical_function_type->parameter_types[parameter_index];
 12911:                             IrType* parameter_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, parameter_type_id);
 12912:                             if (codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, parameter_type_id))
 12913:                             {
 17933:                                 u64 part_size = BUSTER_MIN((u64)8, stored_type->layout.size - part_offset);
 17934:                                 u64 part_copied = 0;
 17935:                                 while (part_copied < part_size)
 17936:                                 {
 17937:                                     u64 remaining = part_size - part_copied;
 17938:                                     u32 copy_offset = part_offset + (u32)part_copied;
 17939:                                     u32 chunk =
 17940:                                         codegen_canonical_copy_chunk(remaining, value_offsets[instruction->operands[1].value] + copy_offset, copy_offset);
 17941:                                     a64_emit_load_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[1].value] + copy_offset, chunk);
 17942:                                     if (indirect)
 17943:                                     {
 17944:                                         a64_emit_store_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 10, copy_offset, chunk);
 17945:                                     }
 17946:                                     else
 17947:                                     {
 17948:                                         a64_emit_store_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value] + copy_offset, chunk);
 17949:                                     }
 17950:                                     part_copied += chunk;
 17951:                                 }
 17952:                             }
 17953:                         }
 17954:                         else if (indirect)
 17955:                         {
 17956:                             if (!stored_type || !stored_type->layout.resolved ||
 17957:                                 (stored_type->layout.size != 1 && stored_type->layout.size != 2 && stored_type->layout.size != 4 &&
 17958:                                  stored_type->layout.size != 8))
 17959:                             {
 17960:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 17961:                                 return result;
 17962:                             }
 17963:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[1]);
 17964:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
 17965:                             a64_emit_store_pointer(&buffer, 10, 9, (u32)stored_type->layout.size);
 17966:                         }
 17967:                         else
 17968:                         {
 17969:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[1]);
 17970:                             u32 place_offset = value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value];
 17971:                             if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, place_offset, 8, true, false))
 17972:                             {
 17973:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
 17974:                                 return result;
 17975:                             }
 17976:                         }
 17977:                     }
 17978:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_READ_MODIFY_WRITE)
 17979:                     {
 17980:                         IrValue* place = function->values + instruction->operands[0].value;
 17981:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
 17982:                         IrType* value_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 17983:                         bool indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
 17984:                                         definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE ||
 17985:                                         (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && place->alignment > 16);
 17986:                         bool pointer_arithmetic = value_type && value_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER &&
 17987:                                                   (instruction->atomic_operation == IR_ATOMIC_ADD || instruction->atomic_operation == IR_ATOMIC_SUBTRACT);
 17988:                         if (value_type && value_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->layout.resolved && value_type->bit_width == 128 &&
 17989:                             value_type->layout.size == 16 && instruction->atomic_operation < IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT)
 17990:                         {
 17991:                             // The sixteen-byte read-modify-write: an exclusive
 17992:                             // pair loop whose operand pair reloads from its
 17993:                             // frame slot each retry, because the arithmetic
 17994:                             // writes the operand registers and a store inside
 17995:                             // the window could clear the monitor while a load
 17996:                             // cannot. The carry rides ADDS/ADC and SUBS/SBC;
 17997:                             // the old value survives in x9:x14 for the result.
 17998:                             if (indirect)
 17999:                             {
 18000:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
 18001:                             }
 18002:                             else
 18003:                             {
 18004:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
 18005:                             }
 18006:                             bool pair_acquire = instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_CONSUME ||
 18007:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE ||
 18008:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE ||
 18009:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
 18010:                             bool pair_release = instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_RELEASE ||
 18011:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE ||
 18012:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
 18013:                             u32 pair_retry_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 18014:                             a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_load_pair(&buffer, 9, 14, 10, pair_acquire);
 18015:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 11, instruction->operands[1]);
 18016:                             c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 12, instruction->operands[1]);
 18017:                             switch (instruction->atomic_operation)
 18018:                             {
 18019:                             case IR_ATOMIC_ADD:
 18020:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xab0b012b));
 18021:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x9a0c01cc));
 18022:                                 break;
 18023:                             case IR_ATOMIC_SUBTRACT:
 18024:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xeb0b012b));
 18025:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xda0c01cc));
 18026:                                 break;
 18027:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_AND:
 18028:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x8a0b012b));
 18029:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x8a0c01cc));
 18030:                                 break;
 18031:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_OR:
 18032:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xaa0b012b));
 18033:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xaa0c01cc));
 18034:                                 break;
 18035:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_XOR:
 18036:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xca0b012b));
 18037:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xca0c01cc));
 18038:                                 break;
 18039:                             case IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE:
 18040:                                 break;
 18041:                             case IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT:
 18042:                                 break;
 18043:                             }
 18044:                             a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_store_pair(&buffer, 13, 11, 12, 10, pair_release);
 18045:                             a64_emit_exclusive_retry(&buffer, pair_retry_offset);
 18046:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 18047:                             c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 14, result_offset);
 18048:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 18049:                             continue;
 18050:                         }
 18051:                         if (!value_type ||
 18052:                             (!pointer_arithmetic && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
 18053:                              (instruction->atomic_operation != IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE ||
 18054:                               (value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_POINTER))) ||
 18055:                             !value_type->layout.resolved ||
 18056:                             (value_type->layout.size != 1 && value_type->layout.size != 2 && value_type->layout.size != 4 && value_type->layout.size != 8) ||
 18057:                             instruction->atomic_operation >= IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT)
 18058:                         {
 18059:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 18060:                             return result;
 18061:                         }
 18062:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 11, instruction->operands[1]);
 18063:                         if (indirect)
 18064:                         {
 18065:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
 18066:                         }
 18067:                         else
 18068:                         {
 18069:                             codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
 18070:                         }
 18071:                         bool acquire = instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_CONSUME || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE ||
 18072:                                        instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
 18073:                         bool release = instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_RELEASE || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE ||
 18074:                                        instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
 18075:                         u32 retry_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 18076:                         a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_load(&buffer, 9, 10, (u32)value_type->layout.size, acquire);
 18077:                         u32 operation = 0;
 18078:                         bool wide = value_type->layout.size == 8;
 18079:                         switch (instruction->atomic_operation)
 18080:                         {
 18081:                         case IR_ATOMIC_ADD:
 18082:                             operation = wide ? UINT32_C(0x8b0b012c) : UINT32_C(0x0b0b012c);
 18083:                             break;
 18084:                         case IR_ATOMIC_SUBTRACT:
 18085:                             operation = wide ? UINT32_C(0xcb0b012c) : UINT32_C(0x4b0b012c);
 18086:                             break;
 18087:                         case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_AND:
 18088:                             operation = wide ? UINT32_C(0x8a0b012c) : UINT32_C(0x0a0b012c);
 18089:                             break;
 18090:                         case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_OR:
 18091:                             operation = wide ? UINT32_C(0xaa0b012c) : UINT32_C(0x2a0b012c);
 18092:                             break;
 18093:                         case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_XOR:
 18094:                             operation = wide ? UINT32_C(0xca0b012c) : UINT32_C(0x4a0b012c);
 18095:                             break;
 18096:                         case IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE:
 18097:                             operation = wide ? UINT32_C(0xaa0b03ec) : UINT32_C(0x2a0b03ec);
 18098:                             break;
 18099:                         case IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT:
 18100:                             break;
 18101:                         }
 18102:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, operation);
 18103:                         a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_store(&buffer, 13, 12, 10, (u32)value_type->layout.size, release);
 18104:                         s64 retry_displacement = (s64)retry_offset - (s64)buffer.count;
 18105:                         if (retry_displacement % 4 || retry_displacement / 4 < -INT64_C(0x40000) || retry_displacement / 4 > INT64_C(0x3ffff))
 18106:                         {
 18107:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
 18108:                             return result;
 18109:                         }
 18110:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x35000000) | (((u32)(retry_displacement / 4) & UINT32_C(0x7ffff)) << 5) | 13);
 18111:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 18112:                     }
 18113:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_COMPARE_EXCHANGE)
 18114:                     {
 18115:                         IrValue* place = function->values + instruction->operands[0].value;
 18116:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
 18117:                         IrType* value_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 18118:                         bool indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
 18119:                                         definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE ||
 18120:                                         (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && place->alignment > 16);
 18121:                         if (value_type && value_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->layout.resolved && value_type->bit_width == 128 &&
 18122:                             value_type->layout.size == 16)
 18123:                         {
 18124:                             // The sixteen-byte compare-exchange: the expected
 18125:                             // pair reloads inside the loop (the register pair
 18126:                             // doubles as the desired pair on the match path),
 18127:                             // both halves compare through CCMP, a mismatch
 18128:                             // leaves through CLREX like the scalar form, and
 18129:                             // the loop returns the old value pair like every
 18130:                             // other atomic — the frontend owns the success
 18131:                             // comparison and the expected write-back.
 18132:                             if (indirect)
 18133:                             {
 18134:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
 18135:                             }
 18136:                             else
 18137:                             {
 18138:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
 18139:                             }
 18140:                             bool pair_acquire =
 18141:                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_CONSUME || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE ||
 18142:                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL ||
 18143:                                 instruction->failure_memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_CONSUME ||
 18144:                                 instruction->failure_memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE ||
 18145:                                 instruction->failure_memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
 18146:                             bool pair_release = instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_RELEASE ||
 18147:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE ||
 18148:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
 18149:                             u32 pair_retry_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 18150:                             a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_load_pair(&buffer, 9, 14, 10, pair_acquire);
 18151:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 11, instruction->operands[1]);
 18152:                             c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 12, instruction->operands[1]);
 18153:                             // CMP X9, X11; CCMP X14, X12, #0, EQ — NE when either
 18154:                             // half differs; the B.NE displacement patches once the
 18155:                             // desired reloads below fix the loop's length.
 18156:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xeb0b013f));
 18157:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xfa4c01c0));
 18158:                             u32 pair_mismatch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 18159:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x54000001));
 18160:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 11, instruction->operands[2]);
 18161:                             c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 12, instruction->operands[2]);
 18162:                             a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_store_pair(&buffer, 13, 11, 12, 10, pair_release);
 18163:                             a64_emit_exclusive_retry(&buffer, pair_retry_offset);
 18164:                             codegen_canonical_a64_patch_local_branch(&buffer, pair_mismatch_offset, (u32)buffer.count, true);
 18165:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xd5033f5f));
 18166:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
 18167:                             c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 14, result_offset);
 18168:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 18169:                             continue;
 18170:                         }
 18171:                         if (!value_type || (value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_POINTER) || !value_type->layout.resolved ||
 18172:                             (value_type->layout.size != 1 && value_type->layout.size != 2 && value_type->layout.size != 4 && value_type->layout.size != 8))
 18173:                         {
 18174:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 18175:                             return result;
 18176:                         }
 18177:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 12, instruction->operands[1]);
 18178:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 11, instruction->operands[2]);
 18179:                         if (indirect)
 18180:                         {
 18181:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
 18182:                         }
 18183:                         else
 18184:                         {
 18185:                             codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
 18186:                         }
 18187:                         bool acquire =
 18188:                             instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_CONSUME || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE ||
 18189:                             instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL ||
