 11263:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), store_operands,
 11264:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(store_operands)))
 11265:                                     {
 11266:                                         result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11267:                                         return result;
 11268:                                     }
 11269:                                     part_copied += chunk;
 11270:                                 }
 11271:                             }
 11272:                         }
 11273:                         else if (indirect)
 11274:                         {
 11275:                             IrType* loaded_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 11276:                             if (!loaded_type || !loaded_type->layout.resolved ||
 11277:                                 (loaded_type->layout.size != 1 && loaded_type->layout.size != 2 && loaded_type->layout.size != 4 &&
 11278:                                  loaded_type->layout.size != 8))
 11279:                             {
 11280:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 11281:                                 return result;
 11282:                             }
 11283:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
 11284:                             u16 load_width = (u16)(loaded_type->layout.size * 8);
 11285:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand load_operands[2] = {
 11286:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, loaded_type->layout.size <= 2 ? 32 : load_width),
 11287:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RAX, load_width, 0),
 11288:                             };
 11289:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, loaded_type->layout.size <= 2 ? S8("MOVZX") : S8("MOV"),
 11290:                                                                        load_operands, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(load_operands)))
 11291:                             {
 11292:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 11293:                                 return result;
 11294:                             }
 11295:                         }
 11296:                         else
 11297:                         {
 11298:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
 11299:                         }
 11300:                         if (!aggregate && instruction->result.value != IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID)
 11301:                         {
 11302:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 11303:                         }
 11304:                     }
 11305:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX)
 11306:                     {
 11307:                         IrValueId base = instruction->operands[0];
 11308:                         IrInstruction* base_definition = function->instructions + function->values[base.value].definition.value;
 11309:                         IrType* base_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[base.value].canonical_type);
 11310:                         if (base_definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL || (function->values[base.value].category == IR_VALUE_VALUE && base_type &&
 11311:                                                                            (base_type->kind == IR_TYPE_ARRAY || base_type->kind == IR_TYPE_VECTOR)))
 11312:                         {
 11313:                             if (base_definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && function->values[base.value].alignment > 16)
 11314:                             {
 11315:                                 c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, base);
 11316:                             }
 11317:                             else
 11318:                             {
 11319:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand base_address_operands[2] = {
 11320:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
 11321:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
 11322:                                         X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[base.value])),
 11323:                                 };
 11324:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("LEA"), base_address_operands,
 11325:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(base_address_operands)))
 11326:                                 {
 11327:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
 11328:                                     return result;
 11329:                                 }
 11330:                             }
 11331:                         }
 11332:                         else
 11333:                         {
 11334:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, base);
 11335:                         }
 11336:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x8d, instruction->operands[1]);
 11337:                         IrType* index_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type);
 11338:                         IrType* element = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 11339:                         if (!index_type || index_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER || !element || element->layout.size > UINT32_MAX)
 11340:                         {
 11341:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 11342:                             return result;
 11343:                         }
 11344:                         if (index_type->is_signed && index_type->bit_width < 64)
 11345:                         {
 11346:                             String8 extend_mnemonic = index_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSXD") : S8("MOVSX");
 11347:                             if (index_type->bit_width != 8 && index_type->bit_width != 16 && index_type->bit_width != 32)
 11348:                             {
 11349:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12161:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, source_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVD") : S8("MOVQ"),
 12162:                                                                                 threshold_vector, 2, float_feature_input) ||
 12163:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, compare_mnemonic, compare_operands, 2, float_feature_input))
 12164:                             {
 12165:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12166:                                 return result;
 12167:                             }
 12168:                             u32 below_threshold_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 12169:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand below_threshold_branch = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 8);
 12170:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JB"), &below_threshold_branch, 1) ||
 12171:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, subtract_mnemonic, subtract_operands, 2, float_feature_input) ||
 12172:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, convert_mnemonic, convert_operands, 2, float_feature_input) ||
 12173:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), high_bit_load, 2) ||
 12174:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("OR"), high_bit_or, 2))
 12175:                             {
 12176:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12177:                                 return result;
 12178:                             }
 12179:                             u32 skip_direct_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 12180:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand skip_direct_branch = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 8);
 12181:                             u32 direct_conversion_offset = skip_direct_branch_offset + 2;
 12182:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JMP"), &skip_direct_branch, 1) ||
 12183:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, convert_mnemonic, convert_operands, 2, float_feature_input))
 12184:                             {
 12185:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12186:                                 return result;
 12187:                             }
 12188:                             s64 below_threshold_delta = (s64)direct_conversion_offset - ((s64)below_threshold_branch_offset + 2);
 12189:                             s64 skip_direct_delta = (s64)buffer.count - ((s64)skip_direct_branch_offset + 2);
 12190:                             if (below_threshold_delta < INT8_MIN || below_threshold_delta > INT8_MAX || skip_direct_delta < INT8_MIN ||
 12191:                                 skip_direct_delta > INT8_MAX || below_threshold_branch_offset + 1 >= buffer.count || skip_direct_branch_offset + 1 >= buffer.count)
 12192:                             {
 12193:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
 12194:                                 return result;
 12195:                             }
 12196:                             buffer.bytes[below_threshold_branch_offset + 1] = (u8)(s8)below_threshold_delta;
 12197:                             buffer.bytes[skip_direct_branch_offset + 1] = (u8)(s8)skip_direct_delta;
 12198:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12199:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 12200:                             continue;
 12201:                         }
 12202:                         else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_IDENTITY && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
 12203:                                  source_type->bit_width == target_type->bit_width &&
 12204:                                  (source_type->bit_width == 32 || source_type->bit_width == 64))
 12205:                         {
 12206:                             // Two float types with the same representation but
 12207:                             // different identity -- `double` assigned to a
 12208:                             // `volatile double`, which the frontend spells as
 12209:                             // an identity cast because the qualified type is
 12210:                             // its own IrType.  The eightbyte the load above
 12211:                             // already placed in RAX is the whole answer.
 12212:                         }
 12213:                         else
 12214:                         {
 12215:                             result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12216:                             return result;
 12217:                         }
 12218:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12219:                     }
 12220:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_STORE || instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE)
 12221:                     {
 12222:                         IrValue* place = function->values + instruction->operands[0].value;
 12223:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
 12224:                         u32 aggregate_parts = 0;
 12225:                         bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type,
 12226:                                                                                    &aggregate_parts);
 12227:                         IrType* stored_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type);
 12228:                         if (!aggregate && stored_type && stored_type->layout.resolved && stored_type->layout.size > 8 &&
 12229:                             stored_type->layout.size <= (u64)UINT32_MAX * 8)
 12230:                         {
 12231:                             aggregate = true;
 12232:                             aggregate_parts = (u32)((stored_type->layout.size + 7) / 8);
 12233:                         }
 12234:                         bool indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
 12235:                                         definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE ||
 12236:                                         (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && place->alignment > 16);
 12237:                         bool stored_f80_opaque = codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_bytes_cached(
 12238:                                                      f80_cache, program, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type) &&
 12239:                                                  instruction->opcode != IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE && result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V;
 12240:                         if (!stored_f80_opaque &&
 12241:                             codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type))
 12242:                         {
 12243:                             if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE || !stored_type || stored_type->layout.size != 16 ||
 12244:                                 result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V ||
 12245:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_x87_shape_cached(f80_cache, program,
 12246:                                                                                      function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type))
 12247:                             {
 12248:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12249:                                 return result;
 12250:                             }
 12251:                             if (indirect)
 12252:                             {
 12253:                                 c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
 12254:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_copy(
 12255:                                          &buffer, X64_REGISTER_RBP, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[1].value]),
 12256:                                          X64_REGISTER_RAX, 0, &x87_stack_depth))
 12257:                                 {
 12258:                                     result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12259:                                     return result;
 12260:                                 }
 12261:                             }
 12262:                             else if (!codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_copy(
 12263:                                          &buffer, X64_REGISTER_RBP, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[1].value]),
 12264:                                          X64_REGISTER_RBP, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]), &x87_stack_depth))
 12265:                             {
 12266:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12267:                                 return result;
 12268:                             }
 12269:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 12270:                             continue;
 12271:                         }
 12272:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE && aggregate)
 12273:                         {
 12274:                             // See the atomic load above: the width is the place's
 12275:                             // promoted size, the value's own size can be smaller.
 12276:                             IrType* atomic_place_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, place->canonical_type);
 12277:                             u64 atomic_width = atomic_place_type && atomic_place_type->layout.resolved ? atomic_place_type->layout.size : 0;
 12278:                             u64 atomic_stored_size = stored_type && stored_type->layout.resolved ? stored_type->layout.size : 0;
 12279:                             bool aggregate_kind = stored_type && (stored_type->kind == IR_TYPE_STRUCT || stored_type->kind == IR_TYPE_UNION);
 12280:                             if (aggregate_kind && (atomic_width == 1 || atomic_width == 2 || atomic_width == 4 || atomic_width == 8))
 12281:                             {
 12282:                                 // The address goes first: the indirect form of
 12283:                                 // c_x64_atomic_address reloads the pointer through rax,
 12284:                                 // which is where the value is about to live.
 12285:                                 c_x64_atomic_address(&emitter, instruction->operands[0], indirect);
 12286:                                 c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[1]);
 12287:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_keep_low_bytes(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_RAX, atomic_stored_size);
 12288:                                 u16 atomic_store_width = (u16)(atomic_width * 8);
 12289:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand aggregate_store_operands[2] = {
 12290:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_R10, atomic_store_width, 0),
 12291:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, atomic_store_width),
 12292:                                 };
 12293:                                 // Sequential consistency exchanges, exactly as the
 12294:                                 // scalar path below spells it; a weaker order is the
 12295:                                 // plain store an aligned x86 write already is.
 12296:                                 if (buffer.error ||
 12297:                                     !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer,
 12298:                                                                          instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL ? S8("XCHG") : S8("MOV"),
 12299:                                                                          aggregate_store_operands, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(aggregate_store_operands)))
 12300:                                 {
 12301:                                     result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12302:                                     return result;
 12303:                                 }
 12304:                                 instruction_id = instruction->next;
 12305:                                 continue;
 12306:                             }
 12307:                             if (!stored_type || (stored_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER && !aggregate_kind) || atomic_width != 16 ||
 12308:                                 atomic_stored_size <= 8 || atomic_stored_size > 16 || !target_cpu_feature_has(target, TARGET_CPU_FEATURE_X86_CX16))
 12309:                             {
 12310:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12311:                                 return result;
 12312:                             }
 12313:                             c_x64_atomic_address(&emitter, instruction->operands[0], indirect);
 12314:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x9d, instruction->operands[1]);
 12315:                             c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x8d, instruction->operands[1]);
 12316:                             // The high eightbyte of an aggregate shorter than the pair
 12317:                             // is padding; zero it so the stored image is the value's,
 12318:                             // not the slot's residue.
 12319:                             codegen_canonical_x64_keep_low_bytes(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_RCX, atomic_stored_size - 8);
 12320:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand zero_rax_operands[2] = {
 12321:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
 12322:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
 12323:                             };
 12324:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand zero_rdx_operands[2] = {
 12325:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 32),
 12326:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 32),
 12327:                             };
 12328:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand atomic_memory = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_R10, 128, 0);
 12329:                             String8 cx16_features_names[] = {S8("cx16")};
 12330:                             if (buffer.error || !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("XOR"), zero_rax_operands,
 12331:                                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(zero_rax_operands)) ||
 12332:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("XOR"), zero_rdx_operands,
 12333:                                                                      BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(zero_rdx_operands)))
 12334:                             {
 12335:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12336:                                 return result;
 12337:                             }
 12338:                             u32 retry = (u32)buffer.count;
 12339:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand retry_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
 12340:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_attributes(
 12341:                                     &buffer, S8("CMPXCHG16B"), &atomic_memory, 1,
 12342:                                     (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = cx16_features_names, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(cx16_features_names)},
 12343:                                     (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalAttributes){.lock = true}))
 12344:                             {
 12345:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12346:                                 return result;
 12347:                             }
 12348:                             u32 retry_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 12349:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JNZ"), &retry_operand, 1))
 12350:                             {
 12351:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12352:                                 return result;
 12353:                             }
 12354:                             s64 retry_delta = (s64)retry - ((s64)retry_branch_offset + 6);
 12355:                             if (retry_delta < INT32_MIN || retry_delta > INT32_MAX || !buffer.bytes || retry_branch_offset + 6 > buffer.count)
 12356:                             {
 12357:                                 result.error = retry_delta < INT32_MIN || retry_delta > INT32_MAX ? CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY
 12358:                                                                                                     : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12359:                                 return result;
 12360:                             }
 12361:                             s32 retry_displacement = (s32)retry_delta;
 12362:                             memcpy(buffer.bytes + retry_branch_offset + 2, &retry_displacement, sizeof(retry_displacement));
 12363:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 12364:                             continue;
 12365:                         }
 12366:                         if (aggregate)
 12367:                         {
 12368:                             if (!stored_type || !stored_type->layout.resolved)
 12369:                             {
 12370:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 12371:                                 return result;
 12372:                             }
 12373:                             if (indirect)
 12374:                             {
 12375:                                 c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[0]);
 12376:                             }
 12377:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < aggregate_parts; part_index += 1)
 12378:                             {
 12379:                                 u64 part_offset = (u64)part_index * 8;
 12380:                                 u64 part_size = BUSTER_MIN((u64)8, stored_type->layout.size - part_offset);
 12381:                                 u64 part_copied = 0;
 12382:                                 while (part_copied < part_size)
 12383:                                 {
 12384:                                     u64 remaining = part_size - part_copied;
 12385:                                     u32 chunk = remaining >= 8 ? 8 : remaining >= 4 ? 4 : remaining >= 2 ? 2 : 1;
 12386:                                     u64 copy_offset = part_offset + part_copied;
 12387:                                     s32 source_displacement = c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[1].value]);
 12388:                                     s32 destination_displacement = indirect ? 0 : c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
 12389:                                     if (copy_offset > INT32_MAX || (s64)source_displacement + (s64)copy_offset > INT32_MAX ||
 12390:                                         (s64)source_displacement + (s64)copy_offset < INT32_MIN ||
 12391:                                         (!indirect && ((s64)destination_displacement + (s64)copy_offset > INT32_MAX ||
 12392:                                                        (s64)destination_displacement + (s64)copy_offset < INT32_MIN)))
 12393:                                     {
 12394:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12395:                                         return result;
 12396:                                     }
 12397:                                     u16 chunk_width = (u16)(chunk * 8);
 12398:                                     String8 load_mnemonic = chunk == 1 || chunk == 2 ? S8("MOVZX") : S8("MOV");
 12399:                                     u16 load_register_width = chunk <= 4 ? 32 : 64;
 12400:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand load_operands[2] = {
 12401:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, load_register_width),
 12402:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
 12403:                                             X64_REGISTER_RBP, chunk_width, (s64)source_displacement + (s64)copy_offset),
 12404:                                     };
 12405:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, load_mnemonic, load_operands, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(load_operands)))
 12406:                                     {
 12407:                                         result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12408:                                         return result;
 12409:                                     }
 12410:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand store_operands[2] = {
 12411:                                         indirect ? codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RDX, chunk_width, (s64)copy_offset)
 12412:                                                   : codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, chunk_width,
 12413:                                                                                            (s64)destination_displacement + (s64)copy_offset),
 12414:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, chunk_width),
 12415:                                     };
 12416:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), store_operands,
 12417:                                                                               BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(store_operands)))
 12418:                                     {
 12419:                                         result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12420:                                         return result;
 12421:                                     }
 12422:                                     part_copied += chunk;
 12423:                                 }
 12424:                             }
 12425:                         }
 12426:                         else
 12427:                         {
 12428:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[1]);
 12429:                             if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE && instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL)
 12430:                             {
 12431:                                 if (!stored_type || !stored_type->layout.resolved ||
 12432:                                     (stored_type->layout.size != 1 && stored_type->layout.size != 2 && stored_type->layout.size != 4 &&
 12433:                                      stored_type->layout.size != 8))
 12434:                                 {
 12435:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12436:                                     return result;
 12437:                                 }
 12438:                                 if (indirect)
 12439:                                 {
 12440:                                     c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[0]);
 12441:                                 }
 12442:                                 u16 atomic_store_width = (u16)(stored_type->layout.size * 8);
 12443:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand atomic_store_operands[2] = {
 12444:                                     indirect ? codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RDX, atomic_store_width, 0)
 12445:                                               : codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, atomic_store_width,
 12446:                                                                                        c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value])),
 12447:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, atomic_store_width),
 12448:                                 };
 12449:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("XCHG"), atomic_store_operands,
 12450:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(atomic_store_operands)))
 12451:                                 {
 12452:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
 12453:                                     return result;
 12454:                                 }
 12455:                                 instruction_id = instruction->next;
 12456:                                 continue;
 12457:                             }
 12458:                             if (indirect)

 12491:                                 return result;
 12492:                             }
 12493:                         }
 12494:                     }
 12495:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_READ_MODIFY_WRITE)

 12418:                                     {

 12420:                                         return result;

 12452:                                     result.error = buffer.error;

 12454:                                 }

 12623:                         }

 12749:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[1]);
 12750:                             c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[1]);
 12751:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x9d, instruction->operands[2]);
 12752:                             c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x8d, instruction->operands[2]);
 12753:                             String8 cx16_features_names[] = {S8("cx16")};
 12754:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand cx16_memory = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_R10, 128, 0);
 12755:                             if (buffer.error ||
 12756:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_attributes(
 12757:                                     &buffer, S8("CMPXCHG16B"), &cx16_memory, 1,
 12758:                                     (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = cx16_features_names, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(cx16_features_names)},
 12759:                                     (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalAttributes){.lock = true}))
 12760:                             {
 12761:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12762:                                 return result;
 12763:                             }
 12764:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12765:                             c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12766:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 12767:                             continue;
 12768:                         }
 12769:                         if (!value_type || (value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_POINTER) || !value_type->layout.resolved ||
 12770:                             (value_type->layout.size != 1 && value_type->layout.size != 2 && value_type->layout.size != 4 && value_type->layout.size != 8))
 12771:                         {
 12772:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12773:                             return result;
 12774:                         }
 12775:                         if (indirect)
 12776:                         {
 12577:                                 break;
 12578:                             case IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT:
 12579:                                 break;
 12580:                             }
 12581:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand low_operands[2] = {
 12582:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RBX, 64),
 12583:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R8, 64),
 12584:                             };
 12585:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand high_operands[2] = {
 12586:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
 12587:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R9, 64),
 12588:                             };
 12589:                             String8 cx16_features_names[] = {S8("cx16")};
 12590:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand cx16_memory = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_R10, 128, 0);
 12591:                             if (!low_mnemonic.length || !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, low_mnemonic, low_operands,
 12592:                                                                                                   BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(low_operands)) ||
 12593:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, high_mnemonic, high_operands,
 12594:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(high_operands)) ||
 12595:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_attributes(
 12596:                                     &buffer, S8("CMPXCHG16B"), &cx16_memory, 1,
 12597:                                     (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = cx16_features_names, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(cx16_features_names)},
 12598:                                     (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalAttributes){.lock = true}))
 12599:                             {
 12600:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12601:                                 return result;
 12602:                             }
 12603:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand retry_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
 12604:                             u32 retry_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 12605:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JNZ"), &retry_operand, 1))
 12606:                             {
 12607:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12608:                                 return result;
 12609:                             }
 12610:                             s64 retry_displacement = (s64)retry - (s64)buffer.count;
 12611:                             if (retry_displacement < INT32_MIN || retry_displacement > INT32_MAX || !buffer.bytes)
 12612:                             {
 12613:                                 result.error = retry_displacement < INT32_MIN || retry_displacement > INT32_MAX ? CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY
 12614:                                                                                                                  : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12615:                                 return result;
 12616:                             }
 12617:                             s32 retry_delta = (s32)retry_displacement;
 12618:                             memcpy(buffer.bytes + retry_branch_offset + 2, &retry_delta, sizeof(retry_delta));
 12619:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12620:                             c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12621:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 12622:                             continue;
 12623:                         }
 12624:                         if (!value_type ||
 12625:                             (!pointer_arithmetic && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
 12626:                              (instruction->atomic_operation != IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE ||
 12627:                               (value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_POINTER))) ||
 12628:                             !value_type->layout.resolved ||
 12629:                             (value_type->layout.size != 1 && value_type->layout.size != 2 && value_type->layout.size != 4 && value_type->layout.size != 8) ||
 12630:                             instruction->atomic_operation >= IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT)
 12631:                         {
 12632:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12633:                             return result;
 12634:                         }
 12635:                         if (indirect)
 12636:                         {
 12637:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[0]);
 12638:                         }
 12639:                         else
 12640:                         {
 12641:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand address_operands[2] = {
 12642:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
 12643:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
 12644:                                     X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value])),
 12645:                             };
 12646:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("LEA"), address_operands,
 12647:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(address_operands));
 12648:                         }
 12649:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x8d, instruction->operands[1]);
 12650:                         if (buffer.error)
 12651:                         {
 12652:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12653:                             return result;
 12654:                         }
 12655:                         codegen_canonical_x64_asm_load(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_RAX, X64_REGISTER_RDX, 0, (u32)value_type->layout.size);
 12656:                         if (buffer.error)
 12657:                         {
 12658:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12659:                             return result;
 12660:                         }
 12661:                         u16 atomic_width = (u16)(value_type->layout.size * 8);
 12662:                         u32 retry_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 12663:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand move_old_operands[2] = {
 12664:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R8, atomic_width),
 12665:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, atomic_width),
 12666:                         };
 12667:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), move_old_operands,
 12668:                                                                   BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(move_old_operands)))
 12669:                         {
 12670:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12671:                             return result;
 12672:                         }
 12673:                         String8 operation_mnemonic = {0};
 12674:                         if (instruction->atomic_operation == IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE)
 12675:                         {
 12676:                             operation_mnemonic = S8("MOV");
 12677:                         }
 12678:                         else
 12679:                         {
 12680:                             switch (instruction->atomic_operation)
 12681:                             {
 12682:                             case IR_ATOMIC_ADD:
 12683:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("ADD");
 12684:                                 break;
 12685:                             case IR_ATOMIC_SUBTRACT:
 12686:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("SUB");
 12687:                                 break;
 12688:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_AND:
 12689:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("AND");
 12690:                                 break;
 12691:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_OR:
 12692:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("OR");
 12693:                                 break;
 12694:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_XOR:
 12695:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("XOR");
 12696:                                 break;
 12697:                             case IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE:
 12698:                             case IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT:
 12699:                                 break;
 12700:                             }
 12701:                         }
 12702:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand operation_operands[2] = {
 12703:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R8, atomic_width),
 12704:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, atomic_width),
 12705:                         };
 12706:                         if (!operation_mnemonic.length ||
 12707:                             !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, operation_mnemonic, operation_operands,
 12708:                                                                   BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(operation_operands)) ||
 12709:                             !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_atomic_register_memory(&buffer, S8("CMPXCHG"), X64_REGISTER_RDX,
 12710:                                                                                       X64_REGISTER_R8, atomic_width))
 12711:                         {
 12712:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12713:                             return result;
 12714:                         }
 12715:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand retry_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
 12716:                         u32 retry_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
 12717:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JNZ"), &retry_operand, 1))
 12718:                         {
 12719:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12720:                             return result;
 12721:                         }
 12722:                         s64 retry_displacement = (s64)retry_offset - (s64)buffer.count;
 12723:                         if (retry_displacement < INT32_MIN || retry_displacement > INT32_MAX || !buffer.bytes)
 12724:                         {
 12725:                             result.error = retry_displacement < INT32_MIN || retry_displacement > INT32_MAX ? CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY
 12726:                                                                                                              : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12727:                             return result;
 12728:                         }
 12729:                         s32 retry_delta = (s32)retry_displacement;
 12730:                         memcpy(buffer.bytes + retry_branch_offset + 2, &retry_delta, sizeof(retry_delta));
 12731:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12732:                     }
 12733:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_COMPARE_EXCHANGE)
 12734:                     {
 12735:                         IrValue* place = function->values + instruction->operands[0].value;
 12736:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
 12737:                         IrType* value_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 12738:                         bool indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
 12739:                                         definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE ||
 12740:                                         (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && place->alignment > 16);
 12741:                         if (value_type && value_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->layout.resolved && value_type->layout.size == 16)
 12742:                         {
 12743:                             if (!target_cpu_feature_has(target, TARGET_CPU_FEATURE_X86_CX16))
 12744:                             {
 12745:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12746:                                 return result;
 12747:                             }
 12748:                             c_x64_atomic_address(&emitter, instruction->operands[0], indirect);
 12749:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[1]);
 12750:                             c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[1]);
 12751:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x9d, instruction->operands[2]);
 12752:                             c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x8d, instruction->operands[2]);
 12753:                             String8 cx16_features_names[] = {S8("cx16")};
 12754:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand cx16_memory = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_R10, 128, 0);
 12755:                             if (buffer.error ||
 12756:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_attributes(
 12757:                                     &buffer, S8("CMPXCHG16B"), &cx16_memory, 1,
 12758:                                     (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = cx16_features_names, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(cx16_features_names)},
 12759:                                     (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalAttributes){.lock = true}))
 12760:                             {
 12761:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12762:                                 return result;
 12763:                             }
 12764:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12765:                             c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12766:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 12767:                             continue;
 12768:                         }
 12769:                         if (!value_type || (value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_POINTER) || !value_type->layout.resolved ||
 12770:                             (value_type->layout.size != 1 && value_type->layout.size != 2 && value_type->layout.size != 4 && value_type->layout.size != 8))
 12771:                         {
 12772:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12773:                             return result;
 12774:                         }
 12775:                         if (indirect)
 12776:                         {
 12777:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[0]);
 12778:                         }
 12779:                         else
 12780:                         {
 12781:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand address_operands[2] = {
 12782:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
 12783:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
 12784:                                     X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value])),
 12785:                             };
 12786:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("LEA"), address_operands,
 12787:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(address_operands));
 12788:                         }
 12789:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[1]);
 12790:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x8d, instruction->operands[2]);
 12791:                         if (buffer.error)
 12792:                         {
 12793:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12794:                             return result;
 12795:                         }
 12796:                         u16 atomic_width = (u16)(value_type->layout.size * 8);
 12797:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_atomic_register_memory(&buffer, S8("CMPXCHG"), X64_REGISTER_RDX,
 12798:                                                                                      X64_REGISTER_RCX, atomic_width))
 12799:                         {
 12800:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12801:                             return result;
 12802:                         }
 12803:                         if (value_type->layout.size == 1 || value_type->layout.size == 2)
 12804:                         {
 12805:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand widen_operands[2] = {
 12806:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
 12807:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, atomic_width),
 12808:                             };
 12809:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOVZX"), widen_operands,
 12810:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(widen_operands)))
 12811:                             {
 12812:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12813:                                 return result;
 12814:                             }
 12815:                         }
 12816:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12817:                     }
 12818:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_FENCE)
 12819:                     {
 12820:                         if (!instruction->atomic_signal_fence && instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL)
 12821:                         {
 12822:                             String8 fence_features[] = {S8("sse2")};
 12823:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
 12824:                                     &buffer, S8("MFENCE"), 0, 0,
 12825:                                     (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = fence_features, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(fence_features)}))
 12826:                             {
 12827:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12828:                                 return result;
 12829:                             }
 12830:                         }
 12831:                     }
 12832:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CLEAR_INSTRUCTION_CACHE)
 12833:                     {
 12834:                     }
 12835:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_INTEGER || instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_FLOAT)
 12836:                     {
 12837:                         IrType* constant_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
 12838:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_FLOAT && constant_type && constant_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && constant_type->bit_width == 80)
 12839:                         {
 12840:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80(constant_type) || result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V ||
 12841:                                 instruction->immediate_count != 2 ||
 12842:                                 (instruction->immediates[1] & ~UINT64_C(0xffff)) ||
 12843:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_store_f80_constant(&buffer, result_displacement, instruction->immediates[0],
 12844:                                                                            (u16)instruction->immediates[1]))
 12845:                             {
 12846:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12847:                                 return result;
 12848:                             }
 12849:                             instruction_id = instruction->next;
 12850:                             continue;
 12851:                         }
 12852:                         u64 immediate = instruction->immediates[0];
 12853:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_INTEGER && instruction->immediate_is_negative)
 12854:                         {
 12855:                             immediate = 0 - immediate;
 12856:                         }
 12857:                         u16 constant_width = codegen_canonical_register_is_64_bit(program, instruction->canonical_type) ? 64 : 32;
 12858:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand constant_value =
 12859:                             constant_width == 32 && immediate > UINT32_MAX
 12860:                                 ? codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate((s64)(s32)(u32)immediate, constant_width)
 12861:                                 : codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(immediate, constant_width);
 12862:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand constant_operands[2] = {
 12863:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, constant_width),
 12864:                             constant_value,
 12865:                         };
 12866:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), constant_operands,
 12867:                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(constant_operands)))
 12868:                         {
 12869:                             result.error = buffer.error;
 12870:                             return result;
 12871:                         }
 12872:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12873:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_INTEGER && constant_type && constant_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
 12874:                             constant_type->bit_width == 128)
 12875:                         {
 12876:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand high_constant_operands[2] = {
 12877:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
 12878:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(
 12879:                                     instruction->immediate_count > 1 ? instruction->immediates[1]
 12880:                                                                       : instruction->immediate_is_negative ? UINT64_MAX : 0,
 12881:                                     64),
 12882:                             };
 12883:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), high_constant_operands,
 12884:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(high_constant_operands)))
 12885:                             {
 12886:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
 12887:                                 return result;
 12888:                             }
 12889:                             c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
 12890:                         }
 12891:                     }
 12892:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_VA_START)
 12893:                     {
 12894:                         if (!canonical_variadic)
 12895:                         {
 12896:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 12897:                             return result;
 12898:                         }
 12899:                         u32 va_list_component_count = codegen_canonical_va_list_component_count(program, instruction->canonical_type);
 12900:                         if (!va_list_component_count)
 12901:                         {
 12902:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
 12903:                             return result;
 12904:                         }
 12905:                         u32 gp_count = 0;
 12906:                         u32 fp_count = 0;
 12907:                         u32 stack_parts = 0;
 12908:                         for (u32 parameter_index = 0; parameter_index < canonical_function_type->parameter_count; parameter_index += 1)
 12909:                         {
 12910:                             IrTypeId parameter_type_id = canonical_function_type->parameter_types[parameter_index];
 12911:                             IrType* parameter_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, parameter_type_id);
 12912:                             if (codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, parameter_type_id))
 12913:                             {
 12914:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
 12915:                                 return result;
 12916:                             }
 12917:                             u32 parts = 1;
 12918:                             bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, parameter_type_id, &parts);
 12919:                             if (parameter_type && parameter_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT)
 12920:                             {
 12921:                                 if (fp_count < 8)
 12922:                                 {
 12923:                                     fp_count += 1;
 12924:                                 }
 12925:                                 else
 12926:                                 {
 12927:                                     stack_parts += 1;
 12928:                                 }
 12929:                             }
 12930:                             else if (aggregate ? gp_count + parts <= 6 : gp_count < 6)
 12931:                             {
 12932:                                 gp_count += parts;
 12933:                             }
 12934:                             else
 12935:                             {
 12936:                                 stack_parts += parts;
 12937:                             }

     8: // range-checking branches. Unknown or malformed input is a diagnostic,
     9: // never a guess.
    10: //
    11: // Instruction knowledge is table-driven, not hand-coded per mnemonic: x86
    12: // selection and encoding go through the generated metadata
    13: // (assembly_x86_metadata_select_source_form, x86_64_metadata.h), with the
    14: // assembly_x86_instruction_size/encode paths covering the legacy subset
    15: // and every prefix family up to EVEX/APX; AArch64 parsing walks the
    16: // generated syntax templates and semantic tables (aarch64_syntax.h,
    17: // aarch64_*_semantics.h) and encodes through aarch64_encoding.h.
    18: //
    19: // Layout, in file order; each anchor is a definition to search for:
    20: //   assembly_space .. assembly_symbol_intern       builder plumbing, labels,
------
    27: //                                                  system registers, SIMD
    28: //                                                  spellings, row selection
    29: //   assembly_x86_vector_form ..                    x86 form classification
    30: //   assembly_x86_instruction_size                  and instruction sizing/
    31: //                                                  encoding
    32: //   assembly_x86_metadata_*                        metadata-driven selection
    33: //                                                  and emission
    34: //   assembly_instruction_parse,                    statement recognition and
    35: //   assembly_source_parse                          the parse phase driver
    36: //   assembly_relocation_append,                    the emit phase and the
    37: //   assembly_instructions_emit, assembly_encode    public entry point

 10671: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL void assembly_x86_metadata_append_feature(String8* names, u32* count, u32 capacity, String8 feature)
 10672: {
 10673:     if (names && count && *count < capacity)
 10674:     {
 10675:         for (u32 index = 0; index < *count; index += 1)
 10676:         {
 10677:             if (assembly_word_equal(names[index], feature)) return;
 10678:         }
------
 10679:         names[(*count)++] = feature;
 10680:     }
 10681: }

 10685:                                                                       u32 operand_count)
 10686: {

 10712: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL u8 assembly_x86_metadata_physical_class(AssemblyRegisterClass class)
 10713: {
------
 10714:     return class == ASSEMBLY_REGISTER_GPR       ? BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_GPR
 10715:            : class == ASSEMBLY_REGISTER_XMM     ? BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_XMM
------
 10716:            : class == ASSEMBLY_REGISTER_YMM     ? BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_YMM
 10717:            : class == ASSEMBLY_REGISTER_ZMM     ? BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_ZMM
 10718:            : class == ASSEMBLY_REGISTER_OPMASK  ? BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_MASK
 10719:            : class == ASSEMBLY_REGISTER_TILE    ? BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_TMM
------
 10725:            : class == ASSEMBLY_REGISTER_SEGMENT ? BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_SEGMENT
 10726:            : class == ASSEMBLY_REGISTER_SPECIAL ? BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_SPECIAL
 10727:                                                 : BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_UNKNOWN;
 10728: }

 10992: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL String8 assembly_x86_metadata_mnemonic(String8 mnemonic)

 10995:     if (assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("loopnz"))) return S8("loopne");
 10996:     // GNU/AT&T spellings for the accumulator sign-extension family are
 10997:     // aliases of the Intel mnemonics represented by metadata.
 10998:     if (assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("cbtw"))) return S8("cbw");
 10999:     if (assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("cwtl"))) return S8("cwde");

 11036:     }

 11041: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL u16 assembly_x86_metadata_att_scalar_extend_source_width(String8 mnemonic)
 11042: {
 11043:     if (assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("movzbw")) || assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("movzbl")) ||
 11044:         assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("movzbq")) || assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("movsbw")) ||
 11045:         assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("movsbl")) || assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("movsbq")))
 11046:         return 8;
 11047:     if (assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("movzwl")) || assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("movzwq")) ||
 11048:         assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("movswl")) || assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("movswq")))
------
 11049:         return 16;
 11050:     if (assembly_word_equal(mnemonic, S8("movslq"))) return 32;
 11051:     return 0;
 11052: }

 11147: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool assembly_x86_metadata_suffix_alias(Target target, AssemblySyntax syntax, String8 mnemonic,

 11234: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool assembly_x86_metadata_suffix_applies(AssemblyOpcode opcode, u32 operand_index, u32 operand_count)
 11235: {
 11236:     if (opcode == ASSEMBLY_OPCODE_X86_LEA || assembly_x86_opcode_is_rotate(opcode) ||
 11237:         opcode == ASSEMBLY_OPCODE_X86_MOVZX || opcode == ASSEMBLY_OPCODE_X86_MOVSX || opcode == ASSEMBLY_OPCODE_X86_MOVSXD)
 11238:     {
 11239:         return operand_index == 0;
 11240:     }
 11241:     if (opcode == ASSEMBLY_OPCODE_X86_SHLD || opcode == ASSEMBLY_OPCODE_X86_SHRD)
------
 11250:         return operand_count == 1 ? operand_index == 0 : operand_index + 1 < operand_count;
 11251:     }
 11252:     return true;
 11253: }

 11255: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool assembly_x86_metadata_suffix_width_matches(AssemblyInstructionInfo info, u8 suffix_width,
 11256:                                                                       AssemblyOperand const* operands,

 11269:         {

 13047:     for (u32 left = 0; left < instruction.metadata_operand_count; left += 1)
 13048:     {

 13052:             continue;

 13112:     }
 13113:     return false;

  1829: // XED keeps the ordinary CMPXCHG/CMPXCHG16B memory rows as `nolock_prefix`
  1830: // aliases beside their lock-spelling rows.  The aliases are useful durable
  1831: // identities for machine recipes, but a LOCK byte is architectural only for
  1832: // these three memory forms.  Keep the exception explicit and shape-checked so
  1833: // a generic memory row can never gain an accidental atomic prefix.
  1834: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool buster_x86_metadata_emit_lock_alias_allowed(BusterX86MetadataForm form,
  1835:                                                                       BusterX86MetadataPatternSemantics pattern,
  1836:                                                                       bool has_memory)

  4699:    An atomic aggregate is read and written as a single integer access of its
  4700:    *promoted* width, which is what c_atomic_promoted_layout pads the type up to
  4701:    (#731): a three-byte record is four bytes aligned four so that one four-byte
  4702:    access covers it.  The widths the backends implement that access at are one,
  4703:    two, four and eight bytes everywhere, plus sixteen on x86-64 when `cx16`
  4704:    gives them CMPXCHG16B and on AArch64 always, through the LDXP/STXP
  4705:    exclusive-pair loops -- see the IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_LOAD and
  4706:    IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE branches in codegen.c, which are where the aggregate
  4707:    forms are emitted; the machine selectors refuse the aggregate shapes and fall
  4708:    back to those.  Anything wider would need a `libatomic` lock, and there is
  4709:    none here, so refusing in lowering is what turns an internal code generation
  4710:    failure into a diagnostic that names the type (#762).
  4711:

------
  7183:         // for the 128-bit integer keyword rather than declaring them in a
  7184:         // header, and code that uses 128-bit arithmetic reaches for them
  7185:         // directly (SQLite's decimal and integer-overflow helpers do).  The
  7186:         // keyword itself is already understood, so name it the same way
  7187:         // __builtin_va_list is named above.
  7188:         C_DEFINE_TYPE_MACRO("__int128_t", S8("__int128"));
  7189:         C_DEFINE_TYPE_MACRO("__uint128_t", S8("unsigned __int128"));
  7190:     }

  7192:     C_DEFINE_TYPE_MACRO("__SIZEOF_DOUBLE__", string_format(arena, S8("{u32}"), layout.double_type.size));
  7193:     C_DEFINE_TYPE_MACRO("__SIZEOF_LONG_DOUBLE__", string_format(arena, S8("{u32}"), layout.long_double_type.size));

  7195:     // of this predefined macro.  Keep the value in the compiler's prelude so

  7196:     // an otherwise ordinary system header does not depend on a host compiler

  5203:     }
  5204:     // The System V x86-64 side of the same stack boundary: a sixteen-aligned
  5205:     // argument's stack home is rounded up to a sixteen-aligned offset, and
  5206:     // the machine placement must count the same padding eightbytes the
  5207:     // canonical emitter and clang do — its callee used to read a stacked
  5208:     // __int128 one eightbyte early. The fixture's callees stay inside the
  5209:     // machine subset by forwarding their halves to the one checker that owns
  5210:     // the __int128 shifts, and the census pin below fails if lowering changes
  5211:     // ever push them to the canonical fallback, which would silently retire
  5212:     // this coverage. On an x86-64 Linux host each image also runs.
  5213:     String8 x64_i128_stack_allocators[] = {
  5214:         S8("none"),
  5215:         S8("mir-stack"),

  5217:         S8("quality"),

  5697:             // a direct YMM0 result the way clang and MSVC do; the run proves
  5698:             // the callee's registers and the caller's expectations agree.
  5699:             S8("tests/basic_c_vector_argument_ymm.c"),
  5700:             // The other end of the Win64 width range: single-lane vectors
  5701:             // riding their element's register, sub-eightbyte vectors
  5702:             // returning in XMM0, and __int128 passed by reference and
  5703:             // returned in XMM0. These families used to disagree with clang
  5704:             // in both directions and were only visible when the two sides of
  5705:             // a call came from different compilers.
  5706:             S8("tests/basic_c_win64_narrow_abi.c"),
  5707:             // Vectors past 64 bytes: pieced indirect arguments and the
  5708:             // model-dependent direct-or-hidden-pointer result, at the host's
  5709:             // model here and at the pinned sub-AVX-512 models below.

  7421:     // padding the promotion added -- are lowering's answer rather than the
  7422:     // layout's, and every allocator has to give the same one.  The fixture
  7423:     // bakes in the bytes Clang writes, so a single translation unit is enough
  7424:     // to pin the representation and not merely self-consistency.  `+cx16` is
  7425:     // forced on x86-64 because the sixteen-byte half of the fixture is the
  7426:     // CMPXCHG16B pair: leaving it to CPU detection would make the coverage
  7427:     // depend on which machine ran the suite.
